项目简介This question is required.*
最多 300 个字。描述您的项目、其愿景、解决的问题及其目标受众。 10 页面说明
1. 你在做什么 / What You Are Building

做“合约杠杆交易的保险”：LiqPass（爆仓保）。
You are building LiqPass, a liquidation insurance product for leveraged derivatives traders.

产品形态：参数化短期保险（8h / 24h / 月度）。
Product form: Parametric short-term insurance (8h / 24h / monthly).

触发与赔付：强平 → 用户提交爆仓订单号 → 系统被动判定 → 按规则赔付。
Trigger & payout: Forced liquidation → user submits the liquidated order ID → system passively verifies → payout according to preset rules.

可信交付：只认链上入账事件与交易所只读数据；证据 → 摘要 → 锚定，可复核。
Trust model: Only on-chain premium receipts and CEX read-only data are trusted; evidence → summary → on-chain anchor, all verifiable and auditable.

首发范围：BTC/USDC、BTC/USDT 的高杠杆场景（≥20×），先做深做透再扩品。
Initial scope: High-leverage BTC/USDC and BTC/USDT perpetuals (≥20×). Go deep on this wedge first, then expand to more products.

2. 解决了什么问题 / What Problem It Solves

新增人群：给“怕爆仓”的散户一个预算内亏损上限 → 敢进场、敢持仓。
New user segment: Gives risk-averse retail traders a budgeted loss cap, so they dare to enter and hold positions.

手续费提升：更高下单频率与持仓时长 → 平台交易量与交易费增加。
Higher fees for venues: More frequent trading and longer holding periods → higher volume and fee revenue for exchanges.

散户收益更稳：尾部亏损换成小而确定的保费，资金曲线更平滑（非保本）。
Smoother PnL for users: Converts tail losses into a small, fixed premium, making the equity curve smoother (not principal-guaranteed).

新赛道：为平台/量化/社区提供“衍生品 × 保障”的白标与 SDK，打开保费分成。
New revenue lane: White-label and SDK for exchanges, quant funds, and communities to bundle "derivatives × protection" and share insurance premiums.

信任可验证：一切以事件与证据链为准，减少扯皮与风控灰度。
Verifiable trust: Everything is driven by objective events and evidence chains, reducing disputes and opaque risk decisions.

3. 商业模式 / 如何赚钱

Business Model / How You Make Money

C 端保费：核心收入。
Retail premiums: Core revenue stream.

交易手续费返佣：与 CEX / DEX 合作获得返佣，提高每单有效毛利；让利一定比例给用户。
Trading fee rebates: Partner with CEX/DEX to receive rebates, lifting gross margin per policy; share part of it back with users.

营销费用：渠道佣金/获客成本（CAC）。
Marketing flows: Channel commissions and customer acquisition cost (CAC) economics.

单位经济学目标：赔付率 60–70%，毛利 20–30%；通过限额、等待期、黑名单与敞口控制稳定波动。
Unit economics target: 60–70% loss ratio, 20–30% gross margin, stabilized via coverage caps, waiting periods, blacklists, and exposure limits.

数据背书（BTC 100× 杠杆）：
Data backing (BTC 100× leverage backtest):

样本期 282 天；爆仓日 139 天；爆仓概率 49.29%。
Sample period: 282 days; liquidation days: 139; liquidation probability 49.29%.
3s/5s 窗口爆仓概率一致（49.29%），规则稳健。
3s/5s sampling windows produce the same liquidation probability (49.29%), indicating stable rules.
爆仓日均变动 -0.91%；非爆仓日 +1.06%；最大单日跌幅 -9.74%。
On liquidation days, average move -0.91%; on non-liquidation days, +1.06%; worst single-day drawdown -9.74%.
100× 赔付上限：按定价公式，赔付比例封顶 50% 本金。
100× payout cap: Pricing formula caps payout at 50% of notional principal.
先验换算：p_24h = 49.29%；p_8h ≈ 20.3%（由日先验折算）。
Prior probabilities: p_24h = 49.29%, p_8h ≈ 20.3% (derived from daily prior).

用途：
How the data is used:

依据 -9.74% 配置单日赔付封顶与敞口限额（日 NEP / 赔付上限）。
Use the -9.74% drawdown to set daily payout caps and exposure limits (daily NEP / max payout).
当日跌幅进入“冲击分位”（如 < -1%）时，load 自动上调一档。
When daily drawdown hits "shock quantiles" (e.g. < -1%), the pricing load factor automatically increases.

行为校准：
Behavioral calibration:

散户盯仓/减仓等微观行为降低实际爆仓概率；
Micro behaviors like monitoring and de-leveraging tend to reduce realized liquidation probability.
但“只在陡跌时购买”的逆选择抬高条件概率；
However, adverse selection (buying only on sharp drops) raises conditional liquidation probability.
做法：在先验上引入行为修正系数 β（0.8–1.0），结合实盘与证据包逐步收敛，宁保守、后下调。
Approach: introduce a behavioral adjustment factor β (0.8–1.0) on top of the prior, then update with live data and evidence bundles—start conservative, adjust downward over time.

4. 为什么是你来做 / Why You

正在开发：CheckoutUSDC 已上线 Base（0xc4d1…9709），前端/后端/验证链路已跑通，可演示。
Already building: CheckoutUSDC is deployed on Base (0xc4d1…9709), and the front-end, back-end, and verification pipeline are wired up and demo-ready.

执行力与跨栈能力：前端/后端/合约/验证由同一人统筹，需求→上线闭环可控，迭代快、成本低。
Execution & full-stack ownership: One person orchestrates front-end, back-end, contracts, and verification, keeping the loop from spec → shipping tight, fast, and cost-efficient.

资源与沉淀：主网合约、事件监听、证据与风控曲线等资产已落地，可复用、可复制，形成进入门槛。
Assets already in place: Mainnet contracts, event listeners, evidence formats, and risk curves are already implemented—reusable, repeatable, and defensible.

商业模型清晰：C 端保费 + 手续费返佣 + 渠道分成（20–40%，T+7），单位经济可推演。
Clear business model: Retail premiums + trading fee rebates + channel rev-share (20–40%, T+7). Unit economics can be modeled, not hand-waved.

三方共赢：用户获得新的赚钱机会；平台赚手续费；我们赚保费与返佣，并以限额/等待期/黑名单/敞口管理实现可持续毛利。
Three-way win: Users gain a new way to shape risk/return; venues earn more fees; you earn premiums and rebates while maintaining sustainable margins via caps, waiting periods, blacklists, and exposure controls.

寻求合作：为加速落地，寻找交易所/渠道/量化/审计等伙伴共同推进。
Looking for partners: Actively seeking exchanges, channels, quant teams, and auditors to accelerate go-to-market together.   写中英双语的项目简介，对我的项目，

直接来结论：  

### 1）当前发展阶段  
选这个：**现场 MVP**  

理由你不用写：整体项目已经有完整闭环 demo，只是还没大规模上线，属于“有跑起来的 MVP”。

---

### 2）核心功能 *（英文填写建议）

直接贴下面这一段即可：

> Core features: LiqPass is a parametric liquidation insurance layer for leveraged derivatives traders. Users pay a small stablecoin premium for 8h/24h coverage; if a forced liquidation happens within the coverage window, they submit the order ID and the system passively verifies it using read-only CEX data. Valid events trigger rule-based payouts up to a capped amount.  
> We provide: (1) a web app for retail users to buy coverage and manage claims; (2) APIs/SDKs for Aptos-based perps/DEXs/communities to bundle “derivatives × protection” and share premiums/fees.

---

### 3）链上组件 *（英文填写建议）

这里重点写 Move 合约和链上逻辑：

> On-chain components: a set of Move modules that (1) accept stablecoin premiums and register time-bounded coverage positions; (2) emit structured events for each policy so off-chain verification services can consume them; (3) store verified liquidation results (signed evidence bundle / hash) and enforce payout rules (coverage window, payout cap as % of notional, waiting period, blacklists, exposure limits); (4) manage an insurance treasury and execute payouts in stablecoins on Aptos.  
> Optional extensions: SBT-style policy/claim tokens for auditability, and standardized interfaces so other Aptos dApps can integrate the protection module.  

这样一填，他们一眼能看出：你有跑起来的 MVP、清晰的核心功能、以及在 Aptos 上具体要落地哪些链上模块。

> - Implement basic risk controls (per-user / per-day limits, payout caps, blacklists, simple pricing curves).  
> - Release a lightweight SDK / API so Aptos perps, DEXs, and communities can integrate “Buy protection” in their UIs.  
> **Timeline:** Weeks 6–12  
>   
> **M3: Mainnet MVP launch with first partners**  
> - Harden contracts and infra (tests, monitoring, external review where possible) and deploy mainnet version.  
> - Onboard 1–2 Aptos ecosystem partners (perp venue or trading community) into pilot integrations.  
> - Run guarded mainnet with conservative limits, collecting real usage and loss-ratio data.  
> **Timeline:** Weeks 12–20  
>   
> **M4: Optimization, growth & ecosystem reporting**  
> - Refine pricing / risk parameters based on live data; add configuration tools for partners.  
> - Expand to additional markets (more pairs / venues) and run co-marketing with early partners.  
> - Publish a public report to Aptos Foundation on KPIs: unique wallets, covered notional, stablecoin TVL impacted, and retention uplift vs. unprotected traders.  
> **Timeline:** Weeks 20–24  

这样结构清晰、有时间表，又能对得上你前面写的目标和路线图。

---

## 中文版推荐信

致：OKX 投资与战略合作团队  
主题：关于投资与战略合作机会——LiqPass（爆仓保）

尊敬的 OKX 团队：

您好！我是 **LiqPass（爆仓保）** 的创始人 **[你的名字]**。LiqPass 正在构建面向 **合约杠杆交易者的参数化爆仓保险层**，目前已在链上跑通从「支付保费 → 记录保单 → 接入交易所只读数据 → 生成证据 → 计算赔付」的端到端闭环。

### 1. 我们在解决什么问题

对 OKX 这类顶级衍生品平台来说，最大的增长仍然来自合约与高杠杆交易。但对于大量小本金散户来说：

- 一次爆仓就足以把他们永远赶出杠杆市场；
- 他们缺乏一个**简单透明的“亏损上限”工具**，不敢长期留在合约生态；
- 社区、KOL、量化团队希望带用户做合约，却没有可复用的风控与保障模块。

LiqPass 的目标是：  
**把“爆仓”这种尾部风险，变成一笔可定价、可分成的小额保费。**

### 2. LiqPass 的方案

- 面向高杠杆合约用户（例如 BTC/USDT 永续 ≥20x），提供 8h / 24h / 月度保障；
- 用户用稳定币支付小额保费，获得「预算内亏损上限」；
- 若在保障期内发生强平，用户提交订单号，我们通过 OKX 只读 API 和证据链系统进行被动验证；
- 满足规则的事件，触发定额或封顶赔付，所有过程以链上事件和证据包为准，可审计、可复核。

我们已在另一条链完成合约、前后端与验证服务的 MVP，并正在申请多家公链基金，将 LiqPass 迁移与扩展到更多生态。

### 3. 为什么 LiqPass 适合与 OKX 绑定

对 OKX 而言，LiqPass 可以带来的价值是直接而可量化的：

1. **提高交易量与手续费收入**  
   有了“爆仓保”，散户更敢加杠杆、敢持仓，从而提升开仓频率与持仓时长，带来更高的合约交易量与手续费。

2. **吸引并留住“小本金新用户”**  
   许多用户本金有限，但有强烈赚钱愿望，又害怕爆仓。LiqPass 给他们一个安全阈值，让 OKX 成为他们第一次认真玩杠杆的首选平台。

3. **新的收入分成渠道**  
   LiqPass 可作为 **白标或嵌入式保险模块** 集成在 OKX 交易界面中，保费按约定比例与平台、渠道、社区进行分成，形成一条全新的收入曲线，而非成本中心。

4. **合规与品牌层面的加分项**  
   可验证、可审计的参数化保险，有助于 OKX 在“用户保护”“风险披露”“产品创新”等方面形成差异化形象。

### 4. 合作与投资诉求

基于上述契合度，我们希望与 OKX 探讨：

- 由 OKX Ventures 或相关团队对 LiqPass 进行 **股权 / 代币投资**，支持我们在合约保险方向上加速迭代；
- 与 OKX 衍生品产品团队探索 **深度集成**：在 OKX 的交易页面内接入 LiqPass 风险保障入口，或共同推出「OKX × LiqPass」联合品牌方案；
- 在风控、数据接口、合规与全球市场方面获得 OKX 的经验支持与资源协同。

项目代码与演示目前开放在：  
- GitHub：`https://github.com/wjz5788/LiqPass`  
- Demo 站点：`https://wjz5788.com/`  （不断迭代中）

期待有机会向贵团队做一次更完整的产品和技术演示，详细汇报我们的数据假设、风控模型以及与 OKX 的具体对接思路。

感谢您的时间与考虑！

此致  
敬礼  

**[你的名字]**  
LiqPass（爆仓保） 创始人  
邮箱：[你的邮箱]  
Telegram / X：[你的账号]

---

## English Version – Recommendation / Intro Letter to OKX

To: OKX Ventures & Strategic Partnerships Team  
Subject: Investment and Strategic Partnership Opportunity – LiqPass (Liquidation Insurance)

Dear OKX Team,

My name is **[Your Name]**, and I am the founder of **LiqPass**, a parametric **liquidation insurance layer for leveraged derivatives traders**. We are building an end-to-end, verifiable workflow that goes from “premium paid” to “policy recorded” to “CEX read-only data + evidence bundle” to “rule-based payout,” anchored on-chain.

### 1. The problem we are solving

For a leading derivatives venue like OKX, growth still heavily depends on leverage and perpetual markets. However, for many small-retail traders:

- One liquidation event is enough to wipe out their account and push them away from leverage forever;  
- They lack a **simple, transparent way to cap losses**, so they are afraid to stay active in perps;  
- Communities, KOLs, and quant teams want to bring users into derivatives but have no reusable risk-control/insurance module.

LiqPass aims to **turn liquidation tail risk into a priced, shareable insurance premium**.

### 2. What LiqPass does

- Target: high-leverage perp users (e.g., BTC/USDT perps ≥ 20x) with 8h / 24h / monthly coverage;  
- Users pay a small stablecoin premium to get a “budgeted loss cap”;  
- If liquidation happens during the coverage window, the user submits the order ID; we passively verify it via OKX read-only APIs and our evidence chain;  
- Valid events trigger fixed or capped payouts, fully driven by on-chain records and verifiable evidence bundles.

We already have an MVP live on another chain (contracts + frontend + verification backend) and are applying for several ecosystem grants to port and extend LiqPass to more environments.

### 3. Why LiqPass is a strong fit for OKX

For OKX, LiqPass is designed to create **direct, measurable value**:

1. **Higher trading volume and fee revenue**  
   With a clear loss cap, retail traders are more willing to take leverage and hold positions longer, which translates into higher perpetual volume and fees for OKX.

2. **Acquire and retain “small-account” users**  
   There is a large group of users with small principals and strong risk appetite, but they are scared of liquidation. LiqPass gives them a safety threshold and makes OKX the natural first choice for “safe leverage.”

3. **New revenue-sharing line**  
   LiqPass can run as a **white-label or embedded insurance module** inside the OKX trading UI, with premiums shared between LiqPass, OKX, and partners/communities according to a clear rev-share model – a new P&L line, not a cost center.

4. **Brand and compliance upside**  
   A verifiable, auditable parametric protection layer helps OKX stand out on “user protection,” “risk transparency,” and “product innovation.”

### 4. What we are looking for

Given this alignment, we would like to explore:

- **Equity / token investment** from OKX Ventures or the relevant team, to accelerate LiqPass as a dedicated “protection layer” for leveraged trading;  
- A **deep product integration** with OKX’s derivatives stack, such as a “Buy Protection” entry directly on the trading interface, or a co-branded “OKX × LiqPass” product;  
- Support on risk, data interfaces, compliance considerations, and go-to-market in key regions.

Our public materials are here:  
- GitHub: `https://github.com/wjz5788/LiqPass`  
- Demo site: `https://wjz5788.com/`  (under active iteration)

I would be grateful for the chance to walk your team through a more detailed product and technical demo, including our data assumptions, risk curves, and concrete integration paths with OKX.

Thank you for your time and consideration.

Best regards,  

**[Your Name]**  
Founder, LiqPass  
Email: [your email]  
Telegram / X: [your handle]

---

你可以把中英文拆成两个文件发，也可以一封邮件里先英文后中文。发之前只要把「[你的名字] / 邮箱 / 账号」这些位置替换掉，就能直接用了。

## 中文版推荐信（给 OKX）

致：OKX 投资与战略合作团队  
主题：关于投资与战略合作机会——LiqPass（爆仓保）

尊敬的 OKX 团队：

您好！我是 **zms**，**LiqPass（爆仓保）** 的创始人。LiqPass 正在构建面向 **合约杠杆交易者的参数化爆仓保险层**，目前已在链上跑通从「支付保费 → 记录保单 → 接入交易所只读数据 → 生成证据 → 计算赔付」的端到端闭环。

### 1. 我们在解决的问题

对于大量小本金散户来说，一次爆仓就足以把他们永远赶出杠杆市场，他们缺乏一个**简单透明的“亏损上限”工具**，不敢长期留在合约生态；社区、KOL、量化团队也缺少可复用的风控与保障模块。

LiqPass 的目标是：把「爆仓」这种尾部风险，变成一笔可定价、可分成的小额保费。

### 2. LiqPass 的方案

- 面向高杠杆合约用户（如 BTC/USDT 永续 ≥20x），提供 8h / 24h / 月度保障；  
- 用户用稳定币支付小额保费，获得「预算内亏损上限」；  
- 保障期内若发生强平，用户提交订单号，我们通过 OKX 只读 API 和证据链系统进行被动验证；  
- 满足规则的事件触发定额或封顶赔付，所有过程以链上事件和证据包为准，可审计、可复核。

我们已在另一条链完成合约、前后端与验证服务的 MVP，并计划将 LiqPass 迁移和扩展到更多生态。

### 3. 为什么适合与 OKX 绑定

对 OKX 而言，LiqPass 可以带来的价值是直接且可量化的：

1. **提升交易量与手续费**：有了“爆仓保”，散户更敢加杠杆、敢持仓，提升合约交易量和手续费收入。  
2. **拉新并留存小本金用户**：给本金有限、怕爆仓的用户一个安全阈值，让 OKX 成为他们第一次认真玩杠杆的首选平台。  
3. **新增收入分成渠道**：LiqPass 可作为白标或嵌入式模块集成在交易界面中，保费按约定比例分成，形成新的收入曲线。  
4. **品牌与合规加分**：可验证、可审计的参数化保险，有助于 OKX 在“用户保护”“风险透明”上形成差异化优势。

### 4. 合作与投资诉求

我们希望与 OKX 探讨：

- 由 OKX Ventures 或相关团队对 LiqPass 进行 **股权 / 代币投资**，加速在合约保险方向的迭代；  
- 与衍生品产品团队探索 **深度集成**：在 OKX 交易页面内接入 LiqPass 保障入口，或推出「OKX × LiqPass」联合品牌方案；  
- 在风控、数据接口、合规与全球市场方面，获得 OKX 的经验与资源支持。

项目资料与演示：  
- GitHub：`https://github.com/wjz5788/LiqPass`  
- Demo：`https://wjz5788.com/`  

期待有机会向贵团队做一次更完整的产品与技术演示，详细汇报我们的数据假设、风控模型以及与 OKX 的具体对接思路。

感谢您的时间与考虑！

此致  
敬礼  

**zms**  
LiqPass（爆仓保） 创始人  
X（Twitter）：**@CryptoNaxin**  
Telegram：**https://t.me/mosheng1u**

## English Version (for OKX Ventures/BD)

To: OKX Ventures & Strategic Partnerships Team  
Subject: Investment and Strategic Partnership Opportunity – LiqPass (Liquidation Insurance)

Dear OKX Team,

My name is **zms**, and I am the founder of **LiqPass**, a parametric **liquidation insurance layer for leveraged derivatives traders**. We have built an end-to-end verifiable pipeline from “premium paid” to “policy recorded” to “CEX read-only data + evidence bundle” to “rule-based payout,” anchored on-chain.

### 1. Problem

For many small-account retail traders, a single liquidation is enough to wipe them out and keep them away from leverage forever. They have no **simple, transparent way to cap losses**, and communities / KOLs / quant teams lack a reusable protection module when onboarding users into perps.

LiqPass aims to **turn liquidation tail risk into a priced, shareable insurance premium.**

### 2. What LiqPass does

- Targets high-leverage perp users (e.g., BTC/USDT perps ≥ 20x) with 8h / 24h / monthly coverage;  
- Users pay a small stablecoin premium to get a clear, “budgeted” loss cap;  
- If liquidation happens during the coverage window, they submit the order ID; we passively verify it via OKX read-only APIs and our evidence chain service;  
- Valid events trigger fixed or capped payouts, driven entirely by on-chain records and auditable evidence bundles.

We already run an MVP on another chain (contracts + frontend + verification backend) and plan to port and extend LiqPass across ecosystems.

### 3. Why LiqPass fits OKX

LiqPass is designed to create **direct, measurable value** for OKX:

1. **Higher trading volume and fee revenue**: With a clear loss cap, retail traders are more willing to take leverage and hold positions longer.  
2. **Acquire and retain “small-account” users**: We give capital-constrained but ambitious users a safety threshold, making OKX the natural first place to try “safer leverage.”  
3. **New revenue-sharing line**: LiqPass can run as a white-label or embedded protection module inside the OKX UI, with premiums shared between OKX, LiqPass, and partners.  
4. **Brand and compliance upside**: A verifiable, auditable parametric protection layer strengthens OKX’s story around user protection, risk transparency, and product innovation.

### 4. What we’re looking for

We would love to explore:

- **Equity / token investment** from OKX Ventures to accelerate LiqPass as a dedicated protection layer for leverage;  
- A **deep product integration** with OKX derivatives, such as a “Buy Protection” entry directly on the trading interface or an “OKX × LiqPass” co-branded product;  
- Support on risk, data interfaces, compliance considerations, and go-to-market in key regions.

Public materials:  
- GitHub: `https://github.com/wjz5788/LiqPass`  
- Demo: `https://wjz5788.com/`  

I would be happy to present a more detailed product and technical demo to your team, including our data assumptions, risk curves, and concrete integration paths with OKX.

Thank you for your time and consideration.

Best regards,  

**zms**  
Founder, LiqPass  
X (Twitter): **@CryptoNaxin**  
Telegram: **https://t.me/mosheng1u**

### 1）你想解决什么问题？你的解决方案是什么？  
（建议用英文填写，我先给英文，再给对应中文）

**英文版：**

> We are solving a simple but painful problem in Web3: retail leveraged traders have no clear, budgeted loss cap. One bad liquidation on a CEX or perp DEX can wipe out a small account and push users away from trading completely. Exchanges, perps protocols and communities also lack a plug-and-play, verifiable “liquidation insurance” module they can offer to users and share fees on.  
>   
> LiqPass is a parametric liquidation-insurance layer for leveraged traders, designed to sit between CEX venues like Binance and the on-chain ecosystem (BNB Chain and other EVMs). Users pay a small stablecoin premium for 8h / 24h coverage; if a forced liquidation happens in that window, they submit the order ID, and we passively verify it via read-only exchange APIs plus an evidence bundle anchored on-chain. Valid events trigger rule-based, capped payouts. For Binance / BNB Chain this becomes a reusable “risk primitive”: a way to attract small-account users, increase stablecoin activity and volume, and give partners a new revenue line from shared premiums.

**对应中文理解版（不用填）：**

我们要解决的是：散户做杠杆，没有一个简单的“预算内亏损上限”；一次爆仓就把小账户直接清空，平台和社区也缺少可复用的爆仓保险模块。  
LiqPass 做的是参数化爆仓保险层：用户用稳定币买 8h / 24h 保障期，期间如果发生强平，就用订单号触发被动验证，通过交易所只读 API + 链上证据包来认定事件，再按规则封顶赔付。对 Binance / BNB 来说，这是一个可复用的风险基础设施，能拉新、拉稳币、拉交易量。

---

### 2）为什么这件事对你如此重要？

**英文版：**

> This matters to me because I don’t want high-leverage trading to be a pure “casino” game where small accounts either get lucky early or get wiped out and quit forever. There is a huge group of users with little capital but strong drive who currently have no safety net and no transparent tools to manage tail risk.  
>   
> Building LiqPass is my way of turning risk management into open, verifiable infrastructure instead of private black boxes. If we can give small traders a survivable path and help major venues like Binance prove that “user protection” is real and measurable – not just a slogan – then we make the whole ecosystem more sustainable. That long-term alignment between users, platforms and builders is what keeps me motivated to work on this for years, not months.

**中文理解版（不用填）：**

这件事重要，是因为我不希望高杠杆永远是“赌场”，小账户要么走运，要么爆仓消失。我想把风险管理做成公开、可验证的基础设施，而不是黑箱。只要能让小散有一条“活下来”的路，又能让像 Binance 这样的平台，把“用户保护”做成真正可量化的东西，这个行业就更健康，这也是我愿意长期干这件事的原因。

**中文版本：**

我们要解决的问题是：散户做合约杠杆，没有一个清晰的“预算内亏损上限”。一次爆仓就能把小账户直接清空，很多原本想赚钱的散户，被吓得不敢进场、不敢长期留在衍生品市场。交易所和社区也缺少一个可复用、可验证的“爆仓保护”模块，无法安心地把这批用户拉进来。

LiqPass（爆仓保）的方案是：为合约杠杆用户提供参数化的爆仓保险。用户用稳定币支付一笔小额保费，买 8 小时 / 24 小时等短期保障窗口；如果在保障期内发生强平，只需要提交订单号，我们通过币安的只读 API 和证据链系统进行被动验证，将结果锚定在链上，符合规则的事件触发按规则封顶的赔付。这样，一方面给散户一个相对可控的“亏损上限”，让原本不敢进场的散户，也有了进场和赚钱的机会；另一方面，对交易所而言，可以提升交易量和手续费收入，让稳定币长期留在 BNB 链上，带来更高的链上活跃度和生态繁荣。

---

**English version:**

The problem we are solving is that retail traders in leveraged derivatives markets have no clear, budgeted loss cap. One bad liquidation can wipe out a small account completely, so many retail users who *want* to make money are simply too afraid to enter or stay in the market. Exchanges and communities also lack a reusable, verifiable “liquidation protection” module, which makes it hard to safely onboard this segment of users.

LiqPass is a parametric liquidation-insurance layer for leveraged traders. Users pay a small stablecoin premium to buy short-term coverage windows (e.g. 8h / 24h). If a forced liquidation happens during the coverage period, they submit the order ID; we passively verify it via Binance read-only APIs plus an evidence bundle that is anchored on-chain, and valid events trigger rule-based, capped payouts. This gives retail traders a more controlled downside and a real chance to participate and earn, instead of being scared off by a single blow-up. For Binance, it means higher trading volume and fee revenue, more stablecoins staying active on BNB Chain, and a healthier, more vibrant on-chain ecosystem.

---

**中文版本：**

最让我骄傲的，不是一笔融资或者某个短期数据，而是：在资源极少、屡次踩坑的情况下，我依然把一个复杂的「爆仓保险」系统，从想法一点点做到了主网可演示的 MVP，而且还在坚持打磨。

之前的项目里，我也试过走捷径：功能做太多、叙事太大、结果都死在「没跑通闭环」之前。这次做 LiqPass，我被迫学会几件事：  
- 先把**最小闭环**跑起来——从「支付保费 → 证据验证 → 赔付决策」这个链条开始，而不是幻想一个完美生态；  
- 接受**反复推倒重来**——后端、合约、前端我都重写过，只要逻辑更清晰、更可验证，就愿意把旧代码全部丢掉；  
- 在技术问题、资金压力、一个人搞全栈的疲劳感之下，仍然保持每周都有看得见的进展，而不是陷在完美主义和情绪里。

这些经历让我意识到：真正有价值的，不是「一次做对」，而是你愿意在一次次打脸之后，继续把产品往「更小、更实、更可验证」的方向收缩。这也是我敢申请 Binance 相关支持的原因——我已经证明自己能在非常有限的条件下，把一条新型风险基础设施做出来，并且愿意长期把它做好。

---

**English version:**

The thing I’m most proud of is not a funding round or a shiny metric, but the fact that I have kept going long enough to turn a very messy idea – “liquidation insurance for leveraged traders” – into a real, mainnet-ready MVP, despite limited resources and a lot of mistakes.

In previous projects I tried to move fast in the wrong way: too much surface, too big a story, and nothing truly closed the loop. With LiqPass, I was forced to learn a few hard lessons:  
- Start from the **smallest verifiable loop** – “premium paid → evidence verified → payout decision” – instead of dreaming about a perfect ecosystem.  
- Be willing to **throw away and rebuild** – I rewrote backend, contracts and frontend more than once; if the model became cleaner and more auditable, the old code went to the trash without regret.  
- Keep shipping through technical setbacks, money pressure, and solo-founder exhaustion – making sure there is visible progress every week, instead of getting stuck in perfectionism or mood.

These experiences taught me that what really matters is not “getting it right the first time”, but being willing to narrow the scope, face reality, and iterate toward something small, real, and verifiable. That’s why I feel confident asking for Binance’s support: I’ve already shown I can build a new piece of risk infrastructure under tight constraints, and I’m committed to doing the slow, unglamorous work to make it actually useful at scale.

---

# LiqPass——面向杠杆交易者的 BNB 链爆仓保险层

## 1. 问题：散户杠杆交易是一场“一次性游戏”

- 小本金散户在中心化交易所（CEX）其实很怕上高杠杆。  
- 一次爆仓就可能把整个账户清空，他们没有一个简单明了的“预算内亏损上限”。  
- 社区、KOL 和量化团队想带用户玩合约，但手里没有**标准化、可复用、可验证**的保障模块。  
- 对交易所和公链来说，这意味着：交易量流失、稳定币 TVL 流失、用户生命周期很短。

---

## 2. 解决方案：LiqPass（爆仓保）

**LiqPass 是一个面向杠杆交易者的参数化爆仓保险层，设计为连接 Binance 与 BNB Chain 之间的“保护层”。**

- 用户在 BNB Chain 上，用稳定币支付一笔小额保费，购买短期保障（8 小时 / 24 小时 / 月度）。  
- 如果在保障期内发生强平，用户提交该笔订单的订单号。  
- LiqPass 通过 Binance 的只读 API 和链下证据流水线，被动拉取并验证这次强平事件。  
- 证据包（标准化数据 + 哈希）会被锚定到链上，满足规则的事件触发**按规则、封顶的赔付**。  
- 整个过程都是事件驱动、可审计的：没有黑箱式的风控决策，也不用靠用户截图扯皮。

---

## 3. 为什么这件事对 Binance 和 BNB 链重要

**对散户用户：**

- 给小本金交易者一个清晰的“预算内亏损上限”，不再是无限下行。  
- 把“爆仓恐惧”变成一笔他们可以接受、提前支付的小额保费。  
- 让高杠杆策略变成“可生存”的玩法，而不是一把 ALL IN 的赌博。

**对 Binance 与 BNB Chain：**

- 吸引并留住原本不敢进场的小本金杠杆用户。  
- 用户因为更有安全感，愿意更频繁地下单、持仓更久，从而提升合约交易量与手续费收入。  
- 让稳定币（USDT/USDC 以及 BNB 生态稳定币）长期在 BNB 链上流动，抬升链上 TVL 和活跃度。  
- 打开一条新的**保费分成收入线**：保险保费可以在 Binance、LiqPass 以及生态合作方之间进行分成。

---

## 4. 当前产品进度（Product Snapshot）

- **已有 MVP 在另一条链上真实跑通：**  
  - 结算合约已部署到主网，负责记录保费支付事件。  
  - 后端与验证服务已接入 CEX 的只读 API。  
  - Web App 已跑通完整流程：购买保障 → 查看保单 → 提交理赔申请。  

- **“证据优先”的设计：**  
  - 每一笔理赔，背后都有标准化的订单数据与衍生指标。  
  - 证据 → Merkle 根 / 哈希 → 上链锚定，方便审计和复查。  

- 本次资助 / 合作，将用于**把 LiqPass 迁移并针对 Binance 与 BNB Chain 做专门优化**。

>（这里可以插入 2–3 张产品截图：产品页、订单页、理赔页。）

---

## 5. 工作原理（高层流程）

1. **在 BNB Chain 上购买保障**  
   用户连接钱包，选择产品（例如：BTCUSDT 50x 24 小时保障），用稳定币支付保费。  

2. **回到 Binance 正常交易**  
   用户在 Binance 上像平时一样开杠杆仓位、交易；LiqPass 只通过只读 API 监听数据，不动用户资金。  

3. **如果发生爆仓 / 强平**  
   用户向 LiqPass 提交这笔订单在 Binance 上的订单号。  

4. **验证与证据生成**  
   链下服务通过 Binance API 拉取该订单及相关交易历史，做标准化处理，生成证据包，并对结果进行签名。  

5. **链上判定与赔付**  
   BNB Chain 上的合约根据保障窗口、规则和证据哈希进行判定；若满足条件，则触发封顶赔付（稳定币）。  

整个流程都基于**客观事件与可复现的数据**，而不是人工拍脑袋的理赔处理。

---

## 6. 商业模式与单位经济

**收入来源：**

- C 端保费：用户为 8 小时 / 24 小时 / 月度保障支付保费。  
- 收入分成：Binance / BNB 上的合约平台 / 社区等合作方参与保费分成。  
- （可选）交易手续费返佣：若与交易量激励计划联动，可获得额外收益。

**风险与毛利（目标）：**

- 目标赔付率（Loss Ratio）：约 60–70%（赔付 / 保费）。  
- 目标毛利率：约 20–30%（考虑风险附加、运营、合作方分成之后）。  
- 风控措施：按用户与按天设置最大敞口、赔付按名义本金比例封顶、设置等待期与黑名单等。

---

## 7. 面向 Binance / BNB 链的路线图

**Phase 1 – BNB 链集成（0–3 个月）**

- 将核心爆仓保险逻辑迁移到 BNB Smart Chain。  
- 部署基础合约：保单存储、事件记录、赔付执行等模块。  
- 搭建针对 BNB 的验证服务，接入 Binance 的只读 API。

**Phase 2 – 公测版本（3–6 个月）**

- 在 BNB 链上线面向散户的公测版 dApp。  
- 先提供一小部分聚焦产品（例如：BTCUSDT 20x / 50x 的 24 小时保障）。  
- 从保守的限额和透明的报表开始，逐步扩大。

**Phase 3 – 深度集成与规模化（6–12 个月）**

- 与 Binance 的部分产品 / 生态合作方做**嵌入式集成**，在交易界面中加入“购买保障”入口。  
- 将覆盖范围扩展到更多交易对与用户群体。  
- 基于真实数据优化定价模型与风险参数。

---

## 8. 为什么是我（创始人）

- 单人创始人，负责前端、后端、智能合约和基础设施的全栈落地。  
- 之前的项目让我意识到：**小而可验证的闭环**，比宏大但模糊的路线图更重要。  
- 已经在另一条链上，用非常有限的资源，跑出一个可用的真实 MVP。  
- 下定决心要把“风险管理”做成开放、可审计的基础设施，而不是黑箱服务。

账号与链接：  

- GitHub：<https://github.com/wjz5788/LiqPass>  
- Demo：<https://wjz5788.com/>  
- X（Twitter）：@CryptoNaxin  
- Telegram：<https://t.me/mosheng1u>

---

## 9. 我们希望 Binance 提供什么

- 战略投资（股权或代币形式），把 LiqPass 打造成专门的“杠杆保护层”。  
- 在产品与技术层面合作，将 LiqPass 风格的保障能力嵌入 Binance / BNB 的使用体验。  
- 在风险、数据接口与市场拓展方面给予支持，一起把更多用户、更多稳定币和更可持续的交易量带入 BNB 生态。

> 给散户一条“活得下来”的杠杆之路；也给 Binance 和 BNB 链一个全新的、可验证的风险基础设施。

---

你可以把这版中文按页面拆开，贴回画布里对应英文的位置，做一个中文版 PDF；也可以做成中英对照版，一页英文一页中文，给投资人和 BD 都看得懂。

---

### 中文版说明（理赔 & 金库安全）

在理赔设计上，LiqPass 采用 **24 小时结清窗口**，而不是无限制的实时自动放款，目的是把「智能合约 / 编辑器 / 代码 Bug 造成金库被瞬间掏空」的风险压到最低。

具体做法：

- **当日理赔，当日记账，次日结清**：  
  所有通过验证的理赔事件，会立刻在前端和透明度面板中记账展示，但实际放款在 24h 结算窗口内统一处理。
- **合约只负责“记录”和“额度控制”，不做无限自动提款**：  
  合约负责记录保费、保单、理赔结果，并限制单日、单笔、单地址的最大赔付额度，超出阈值需要人工/多签复核。
- **24h 风险缓冲**：  
  即使出现智能合约漏洞、编辑器问题或逻辑 Bug，最多只影响一个结算周期内的额度，可以紧急关停、修复和回滚，而不是一瞬间被人把金库榨干。
- **配合透明度看板**：  
  你截图里的「7D 赔付率 / 金库余额 / 每日保费与赔付曲线 / 事件摘要」就是这个设计的一部分：所有出入金都可见、可追踪、可审计。

一句话总结：  
> 理赔上我们宁愿“慢一些、但可控”，用 24h 结清 + 限额 + 透明度，换取金库在极端情况下的可生存性。

---

### English version (Claims & Treasury Safety)

For claims, LiqPass uses a **24-hour settlement window** instead of unconstrained instant auto-payouts. The goal is to minimize the risk that a smart-contract bug, editor mistake, or logic error could drain the treasury in one shot.

Key elements:

- **Same-day accounting, next-day settlement**  
  All approved claims are recorded immediately and surfaced in the UI / transparency dashboard, but actual payouts are processed within a 24h batch window.
- **Contracts record and enforce limits; they don’t allow unlimited withdrawals**  
  On-chain logic handles premiums, policies, claim results, and per-day / per-user / per-policy payout caps. Anything beyond configured limits requires human / multisig review instead of blind execution.
- **24h risk buffer**  
  Even if there is a bug in the contract or code, damage is bounded to a single settlement cycle. The system can be paused and fixed before the treasury is fully drained.
- **Backed by a transparency dashboard**  
  The screens you saw—7D policies sold, loss ratio, treasury balance, daily premium vs. payout charts, and event logs—are part of this design: every inflow and outflow is visible and auditable.

In one line:  
> We intentionally trade a bit of speed for safety—using 24h settlement, limits, and transparency so the treasury can survive even in worst-case scenarios.

---

### 文案（中文）

理赔方面，LiqPass 采用 **24 小时结清** 的方式，而不是无限制的实时自动放款，核心目的是：  
在智能合约、前端编辑器或代码出现问题时，**防止金库在短时间内被瞬间掏空**。

- 当日理赔，当日记账，次日结清：  
  所有通过验证的理赔会立刻在系统中记账，并出现在透明度面板里，但实际放款在 24h 结算窗口内统一处理。
- 合约只负责“记录 + 限额”，不做无限自动提款：  
  合约记录保费、保单、理赔结果，并对单日、单人、单保单的赔付额度做严格上限，超过阈值需要人工 / 多签复核。
- 24h 风险缓冲：  
  即使合约或逻辑存在 Bug，损失也被限制在单个结算周期内，可以及时关停、修复，而不是被人一次性把金库榨干。
- 配合透明度看板：  
  7 日售出保单数、7 日保费、7 日赔付、当前在保份数、每日保费 vs 赔付曲线、事件摘要和每笔链上交易链接，都公开展示，让所有资金流向可查、可审计。

**一句话总结：**  
我们刻意牺牲一点速度，换取金库在最极端情况下的“可生存性”。

---

### English copy（同一页下面放小一号）

For claims, LiqPass uses a **24-hour settlement window** instead of unconstrained instant payouts.  
The goal is to prevent a smart-contract bug or logic error from draining the treasury in one shot.

- Same-day accounting, next-day settlement:  
  Approved claims are recorded immediately and surfaced in the UI / transparency dashboard, but payouts are processed in a 24h batch.
- Contracts record and enforce limits, not unlimited withdrawals:  
  On-chain logic tracks premiums, policies, claim results, and strict per-day / per-user / per-policy caps. Anything beyond configured limits requires human / multisig review.
- 24h risk buffer:  
  Even if there is a bug, damage is bounded to a single settlement cycle; the system can be paused and fixed before the treasury is emptied.
- Backed by a transparency dashboard:  
  7D policies sold, premiums, payouts, active policies, daily premium vs. paid chart, event logs, and on-chain tx links make every inflow/outflow visible and auditable.

**In one line:**  
We intentionally trade a bit of speed for safety so the treasury can survive worst-case scenarios.

---

### Slide title

**Claims & Treasury Safety – 24h Settlement Design**

---

### Main copy (English)

LiqPass intentionally uses a **24-hour settlement window** for claims instead of unconstrained instant payouts.  
The goal is to prevent a smart contract bug, editor mistake, or logic error from draining the treasury in one shot.

- **Same-day accounting, next-day settlement**  
  All approved claims are recorded immediately and shown in the dashboard, but payouts are processed in a 24h batch cycle.

- **Contracts record and enforce limits, not unlimited withdrawals**  
  On-chain logic tracks premiums, policies, and claim results, and enforces strict per-day / per-user / per-policy caps. Any payout beyond those limits requires manual or multisig review.

- **24h risk buffer**  
  Even if there is a contract or logic bug, the maximum damage is bounded to a single settlement window. The system can be paused, patched, and resumed before the treasury is emptied.

- **Full transparency for every inflow and outflow**  
  A public dashboard exposes 7D policies sold, 7D premiums, 7D payouts, active policies, daily premium vs. paid curves, plus a chronological event log. Each event links to the underlying on-chain transaction.

**In one sentence:**  
> We trade a little speed for a lot of safety—24h settlement + limits + transparency make the treasury survivable even in worst-case scenarios.

---

### Screenshot layout (captions in English)

You can add these as small labels under each image:

1. **Product Center** – Configure principal, leverage, and see real-time premium & payout calculation.  
2. **Orders** – Track each active policy: premium paid, remaining time, and max payout.  
3. **Claims** – Operator view to verify claims, mark payouts, and see countdown per policy.  
4. **Transparency Dashboard** – 7D premiums & payouts, active policies, treasury balance, and full event history.  
5. **On-chain Explorer** – Example transaction showing USDC premium or payout flowing through the Checkout contract.

这样拷贝过去，你的「理赔 + 安全」这一页在英文材料里就很完整了，截图按这五个标题摆就行。

> 2. **提取与转录**：使用工具如“剪映/CapCut/来剪”生成中文字幕并导出SRT，或使用开源工具（如Whisper WebUI）完成转录。 
> 3. **使用“ComeCut”**：它可能已经集成了ASR、翻译和TTS，简化整个流程，避免手动操作。
>
> **[简化步骤和考虑注意点]**
> 用户提到“IndexTTS-Online版本”，可能是指类似“来剪”这种工具。可以按repo描述提到：例如，基于IndexTTS做的轻量视频编辑/翻译工具，支持web/桌面版。关于“IndexTTS2”，支持精确的音视频同步，但目前的demo是否支持该功能不确定。
>
> **[极简流程总览]**
> 这个流程是可行的，涉及三个模块：IndexTTS 负责第三步，STT 和翻译是前两步。以下是简化流程：
> 
> - **步骤 A：** 从视频中提取中文字幕。
>   - 使用在线字幕服务，如自动中文ASR，或上传视频至 CapCut 或来剪进行自动字幕生成（导入视频 → 文本 → 生成字幕 → 导出 SRT）。
> 
> - **步骤 B：** 翻译信息为英文。
>   - 使用翻译工具，如 ChatGPT，翻译时保留逐句对齐，以确保与同步效果。
> 
> - **步骤 C：** 使用 IndexTTS-Online 生成英文配音并与视频合成。
>
</details>

### AI
<!-- mid: a1d8f84c-35f7-43b2-8828-a51c5cb8d076 -->
> 🌐 **引用来源**:
> 1. [indextts · GitHub Topics · GitHub](https://github.com/topics/indextts)
> 2. [IndexTTS 2 Demo - a Hugging Face Space by IndexTeam](https://huggingface.co/spaces/IndexTeam/IndexTTS-2-Demo)
> 3. [index-tts/index-tts: An Industrial-Level Controllable and ...](https://github.com/index-tts/index-tts?utm_source=chatgpt.com)
> 4. [IndexTTS 2 Demo - a Hugging Face Space by IndexTeam](https://huggingface.co/spaces/IndexTeam/IndexTTS-2-Demo)
> 5. [IndexTTS2: A Breakthrough in Emotionally Expressive and Duration-Controlled Auto-Regressive Zero-Shot Text-to-Speech](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.21619?utm_source=chatgpt.com)
> 6. [indextts · GitHub Topics · GitHub](https://github.com/topics/indextts)

可以做到，而且流程其实很清晰：  
**IndexTTS 只负责“英⽂配音”这一步，前面两步要用别的工具来配合。**

我给你一个**线上版最小闭环方案**，你只要能上传文件、下载文件就能跑通。

---

## 总体流程

1. **中文视频 → 中文字幕（ASR）**
2. **中文字幕 → 英文字幕（翻译）**
3. **英文字幕 → IndexTTS-Online 英文配音**
4. **把英文配音塞回原视频，生成英文版视频**

下面每一步给你一个“尽量无代码”的做法。

---

## 第 1 步：视频转中文字幕

目标：拿到一份「时间轴 + 中文文本」的 SRT 或 TXT。

线上方式有三类：

### 方案 A：用带“自动字幕”的剪辑工具（最省心）

比如剪映 / CapCut / 来剪（ComeCut 这类轻量编辑器就是为视频+字幕场景做的，支持 SRT、翻译等功能）citeturn2view0  

通用步骤：

1. 打开网页版/桌面版剪辑工具。
2. 导入你的视频。
3. 找到类似“语音转字幕 / 自动字幕 / 自动识别”的功能。
4. 选择「中文」识别，生成字幕。
5. 导出字幕（优先导出 `.srt`，没有的话复制整段文本也行）。

### 方案 B：用 Whisper 系列在线工具

很多网站提供 Whisper 在线转写（上传音视频 → 自动转中文文本）。核心就是 Whisper 这种 ASR 模型，把中文音频转成文字。

流程类似：

1. 上传视频（或先用编辑器/ffmpeg 把音频抽出来）。
2. 选择语言：Chinese / 中文。
3. 转写完成后导出 SRT / TXT。

> 要求不高的话，A 方案已经够用。

---

## 第 2 步：中文字幕 → 英文字幕

目标：把中文句子翻译成英文，最好一行对应一行，便于后面对齐音频。

做法很简单：

1. 把字幕文件内容复制出来（或逐段拷贝）。
2. 扔到翻译工具（比如直接丢给我，让我“逐行翻译并保留时间轴结构”）。
3. 拿回一份**英文版字幕**（最好仍然是 SRT 格式：保留时间码，只替换文本）。

小建议：

- 翻译时，**优先保证意思清晰 + 语速自然**，不要硬翻到很长的句子，否则 TTS 会变很啰嗦、不好对齐画面。
- 可以先按镜头节奏粗略拆段：1–2 句一段。

---

## 第 3 步：用 IndexTTS-Online 生成英文配音

这里才轮到 IndexTTS 上场。

你可以用官方团队在 Hugging Face 的在线 Demo（IndexTTS-2 Demo）来生成英文语音：citeturn1view0turn0search6  

> IndexTTS 是跨语言零样本 TTS，可以用一小段参考声音克隆音色，再让它说英文台词。citeturn0search1turn0search6  

典型操作流程（以在线 Demo 为例）：

1. **准备一个参考声音（可选但推荐）**
   - 录一段 15–30 秒的你本人中文说话音频，或者你想要的播音员风格音频。
   - 这段音频会告诉 IndexTTS：“模仿成这种嗓音”。

2. **打开 IndexTTS 在线 Demo**
   - 打开 Hugging Face 上的 IndexTTS-2 Demo 页面。citeturn1view0  

3. **按段生成语音**
   - 选一小段英文字幕（例如 1–2 句）。
   - 在 Demo 里上传参考音频（若有此选项）。
   - 把这段英文文本粘贴到输入框。
   - 选择语言/参数（如果有），确保是 English。
   - 点击生成 → 下载生成的 `.wav` / `.mp3` 音频文件。
   - 重复这个过程，把整段视频拆成多段音频（Segment1.wav、Segment2.wav…）。

> IndexTTS-2 本身支持“时长控制”，理论上很适合作视频配音和画面对齐，不过在线 Demo 是否开放这个参数要看具体 UI。citeturn0academia14  

---

## 第 4 步：把英文配音塞回视频

最后一步是视频编辑，不需要高级软件，任何时间轴式剪辑器都行。

例如：

1. 在剪辑软件里导入**原视频**。
2. 导入你生成的每一段英文音频。
3. **静音掉原始音轨**（或者把原中文音量压低，当背景声）。
4. 按时间把每一段英文音频拖到对应位置：
   - 以时间轴 / 字幕时间码为参考。
   - 如果 IndexTTS 说话稍快/慢一点，可以：
     - 微调音频起始时间；
     - 轻微拉伸/压缩音频时长（大部分剪辑软件支持“时间拉伸/变速”）。
5. 导出新视频（H.264 + AAC 这种常见配置就够）。

你就得到一个：**画面不变、语音变成英文、风格由 IndexTTS 驱动**的版本。

---

## 现实中的两个实用组合

如果你懒得折腾太多软件，可以考虑这两种玩法：

### 组合 1：剪辑软件 + IndexTTS Demo（推荐起步）

- 剪映 / 来剪：做  
  - 中文自动字幕  
  - 字幕导出  
- ChatGPT：做  
  - 字幕逐行翻译成英文  
- IndexTTS-2 Online Demo：做  
  - 英文文本 → 英文配音  
- 剪映 / 来剪：做  
  - 把英文配音贴回视频，导出。

### 组合 2：尝试带“视频翻译 + IndexTTS”的开源工具

像 ComeCut 这类项目，本身就是“轻量视频编辑 + TTS + 字幕/SRT + 视频翻译”的一站式编辑器，并且标了 indextts 这个 topic，说明就是往这条路上走的。citeturn2view0  
这类项目一般需要你稍微配置一下环境，但一旦跑起来，可以减少你手动在多个网站来回拷贝的时间。

---

## 小坑提前说清楚

- **英文本比中文更长/短**：文案要略微“口语化 + 不太长”，否则容易对不上嘴型/节奏。
- **分段太粗**：建议按镜头/句子分成 5–10 秒一段生音频，比一次整篇好多了，调节对齐更灵活。
- **版权**：只处理你自己有权使用的视频（你自己的、授权的），别拿别人的影视剧全片去跑 TTS。

---

如果你愿意，我可以下一步帮你：  
直接从「一段中文台词」开始，给你做出**英文版脚本 + 适合 IndexTTS 的分段文本模板**，你只负责往 Demo 里复制粘贴就行。

### AI
<!-- mid: 958df8f1-7eca-4800-9f32-bdbd394e836c -->
First, let me briefly introduce this product.

This product is designed for retail traders who want to use **high leverage**. In other words: people with **small capital but strong desire to “make it back quickly”**. They want to flip their accounts fast, but they’re scared of getting liquidated. Our product is built exactly for them.

For example, what we recommend is:  
you put in **100 USDT of principal**, open **100× leverage**, and pay a **premium of 13.2**. In return, we **cover you for 48**. That’s our basic product.

We are essentially **selling insurance**.  
You still do all your trading on the usual centralized exchanges.  
For the first version, our primary trading pair is **BTC-USDT / BTC-USDC**.

To use our product, you need to **bind an exchange API key**. A lot of people worry that this might violate privacy or that their funds could be at risk. Our approach solves that very cleanly:

- One person can have **multiple exchange accounts**.  
- If your main trading account is on **Binance**, you can bind an **OKX** account to us.  
- If your main account is on **OKX**, you can bind a **Binance** account.  
- Or you can simply bind a small side account on **Gate、Bybit**, etc.

This way, your main account stays safe, and the bound account only needs enough balance to support the insured trades. This perfectly solves the “API key risk” concern in a practical way.

---

### How the product works

The flow is:

1. **Connect your wallet** on our site.  
2. Go to the exchange and **create an API key**.  
3. Come back to our product, **enter the API key** and click **API verification**.  
4. If the verification passes, you’re allowed to **buy the insurance policy**.

After that, you go to the exchange and trade as usual.

Suppose you buy a policy for **100 USDT principal, 100× leverage**.  
Within the coverage window, maybe today you open **5 positions** and all 5 are profitable — no problem, you keep all the profit, we don’t interfere.  
Then on the **6th trade**, you get liquidated.  
For this 6th trade, you can come to us and **make a claim**.

Right now this is a **24-hour product**: once you purchase, we cover you for **24 hours**.  
During that period, if the insured account has a liquidation that meets the rules, you can claim.

---

### What problem are we solving?

We are essentially **creating a new user segment**:

- Retail users who **want** to use high leverage,  
- but **don’t dare** to, because they’re afraid of liquidation.

With LiqPass, they get a **budgeted downside**:  
they know their **maximum loss is capped by the premium + residual risk**, so they’re more willing to enter the market and open positions.

For platforms and ecosystems:

- **Trading fees naturally go up**: more orders, longer holding time, more volume.
- **Retail users get another way to make money**:  
  Previously they only had things like airdrops, launchpads, spot trading, etc.  
  Now they have **“insure my high-leverage trades”** as a new path.

On top of that, some **communities and KOLs** can monetize their influence:

- For example, signal groups / copy-trading leaders can combine their strategies with our insurance product,  
- so that followers dare to use higher leverage under protection and everyone shares more upside.

We also **rebate part of the trading fees** to users:  
- We plan to return around **80% of the trading-fee revenue** to users.  
- Our own revenue comes from:
  - The **premium**, and  
  - The remaining **20% of trading-fee rebates**.

So the business model is quite clear:

1. **Premium income**  
2. **Trading-fee rebates**  
3. **Channel / partner revenue sharing**

At the same time, it’s a **three-way win**:

- Users: get a new way to earn and a cap on their downside.  
- Exchanges: get more trading volume and more fees.  
- We: earn the premium + part of the rebate.

---

### Risk & data side

Our risk pricing uses **historical BTC data over 200+ days**.  
From that data, the liquidation probability under a given leverage and holding window is roughly **around 50%**.

But in practice, the actual results are **a bit better than the raw model**, because:

- Many retail users will actively **watch their positions**,  
- They may **reduce or close** before full liquidation,  
- So the true claim rate can be lower than the naive 50% assumption.

This gives us a **reasonable margin** between premium income and expected payouts.

---

### Current stage

Right now, the product is at a stage where it is **already usable**, but not yet fully rolled out at scale.

Let me walk through a simple demo flow:

1. **Connect wallet**  
2. After connecting, **enter your API key** and the trading pair (e.g. BTC-USDT-SWAP).  
3. Click **“Verify”** — if it passes, the policy becomes active and you can purchase.

For example, here:

- Principal: **100**  
- Leverage: **100×**  
- Premium: **13.2**  
- Coverage: **48** (max payout)

When you click **Buy**, we **write your purchase data on-chain**.  
Once the transaction is confirmed, the purchase is successful.

The order then appears in the **Order Management** page:

- You can see your **coverage time window**,  
- Your **maximum payout amount**,  
- And the **deadline for making a claim**.  
If you don’t claim before the deadline, we won’t pay — this is both:

- A **positive incentive** to avoid abuse / delay, and  
- A **safeguard** against system bugs or edge cases causing late or invalid claims.

You can also click **“View on chain”** to see the on-chain record of your purchase and the smart contract we deployed.  
Currently, we’ve only deployed the **checkout (purchase) contract** and are still doing **manual payout**.

---

### Claim & transparency

When you’re liquidated:

1. Go to the **Payout / Claims** page.  
2. Click **“Verify”**,  
3. Enter your **exchange order ID**.  

We then query the exchange via the **read-only API** and return:

- Whether you were liquidated,  
- On which exchange,  
- Which trading pair,  
- When the liquidation occurred,  
- The PnL, etc.

Right now, **payout is manual**:

- We use this verification result as the **evidence**,  
- Then we process the payout by hand in the next phase.

Next step will be **automated payout**, directly triggered after:

- On-chain policy is valid, and  
- The verification result meets the rules.

For **transparency**, we show a dashboard (currently in simulation mode) with:

- How many policies we’ve sold,  
- Total premium collected,  
- Total payouts made,  
- And how the **premium pool and payouts** are distributed.

This gives both users and partners a clear view of:

- Where the money comes from,  
- Where it goes,  
- And how sustainable the system is.

---

## 16:53 LiqPass概念介绍

来源：6922cb9f-a868-832d-b445-79a9f6d50640
时间：2025-11-23 16:53

### 我
<!-- mid: 3bc54b92-8589-47f0-824d-bf9195061c91 -->
ha，# LiqPass – Liquidation Insurance for Leveraged Traders on BNB Chain              ## 1. Problem: Retail Leverage Is a One-Strike Game &#x20;              - Small-account retail traders on CEXs are afraid to touch high leverage. &#x20;     - One liquidation can wipe out their entire account; there is no simple, budgeted loss cap. &#x20;     - Communities, KOLs, and quant teams want to bring users into perps, but lack a reusable, verifiable protection module. &#x20;     - For exchanges and chains, this means lost volume, lost stablecoin TVL, and short-lived user cohorts. &#x20;              ---              ## 2. Solution: LiqPass (爆仓保) &#x20;              \*\*A parametric liquidation-insurance layer for leveraged traders, designed to sit between Binance and BNB Chain.  \*\*              - Users pay a small stablecoin premium (on BNB Chain) for short-term coverage (8h / 24h / monthly). &#x20;     - If a forced liquidation happens within the coverage window, they submit the order ID. &#x20;     - LiqPass passively verifies the event via Binance read-only APIs and an off-chain evidence pipeline. &#x20;     - The evidence bundle (normalized data + hash) is anchored on-chain; valid events trigger rule-based, capped payouts. &#x20;     - Everything is event-driven and auditable: no opaque risk decisions, no ad-hoc screenshots. &#x20;              ---              ## 3. 我先直接帮你：**整理一版干净的英文正式稿 + 完整中文翻译版**。你可以用来投 Binance / BNB Chain、Binance Labs、各种表单。

## 一、英文整理版（直接可用）

```markdown
# LiqPass – Liquidation Insurance for Leveraged Traders on BNB Chain

## 1. Problem: Retail Leverage Is a One-Strike Game

- Small-account retail traders on CEXs are afraid to use high leverage.  
- A single forced liquidation can wipe out their entire account; there is no simple, budgeted loss cap.  
- Communities, KOLs, and quant teams want to bring users into perps, but lack a reusable, verifiable protection module.  
- For exchanges and chains, this means lost volume, lost stablecoin TVL, and short-lived user cohorts.

---

## 2. Solution: LiqPass (爆仓保)

**A parametric liquidation-insurance layer for leveraged traders, designed to sit between Binance and BNB Chain.**

- Users pay a small stablecoin premium (on BNB Chain) for short-term coverage (8h / 24h / monthly).  
- If a forced liquidation happens within the coverage window, they submit the Binance order ID.  
- LiqPass passively verifies the event via Binance read-only APIs and an off-chain evidence pipeline.  
- The evidence bundle (normalized data + hash) is anchored on-chain; valid events trigger rule-based, capped payouts.  
- Everything is event-driven and auditable: no opaque risk decisions, no ad-hoc screenshots.

---

## 3. Why It Matters for Binance & BNB Chain

**For retail users**

- Gives small traders a clear, budgeted loss cap instead of unlimited downside.  
- Turns “爆仓恐惧” into a known, up-front premium they can afford.  
- Makes high-leverage strategies survivable instead of a one-shot gamble.

**For Binance & BNB Chain**

- Attracts and retains small-account leverage users who would otherwise never enter.  
- Increases perp volume and fee revenue as users trade and hold positions more confidently.  
- Keeps stablecoins (USDT / USDC and BNB-native stables) circulating on BNB Chain.  
- Creates a new fee-sharing line: insurance premiums can be shared among Binance, LiqPass, and ecosystem partners.

---

## 4. Product Snapshot (Today)

- **MVP already running on another chain**
  - Checkout contract deployed on mainnet, emitting premium payment events.  
  - Backend + verifier service integrated with CEX read-only APIs.  
  - Web app flows for: buy coverage → see active policies → submit claims.

- **Evidence-first design**
  - Each claim is backed by normalized order data + derived metrics.  
  - Evidence → Merkle/root hash → anchored on-chain for auditability.

- This grant / partnership will be used to **port and specialize LiqPass for Binance & BNB Chain**.

*(Here you can insert 2–3 screenshots of your current LiqPass UI: product page, orders page, claims page.)*

---

## 5. How It Works (High Level)

1. **Buy coverage on BNB Chain**  
   User connects wallet, selects a product (e.g. 24h BTCUSDT 50x coverage), and pays a stablecoin premium.

2. **Wait & trade on Binance**  
   User trades on Binance as usual with leverage; LiqPass only listens via read-only APIs.

3. **If liquidation happens**  
   User submits the Binance order ID to LiqPass.

4. **Verification & evidence**  
   Off-chain service fetches order/trade history from Binance APIs, normalizes it, builds an evidence bundle, and signs a result.

5. **On-chain decision & payout**  
   BNB Chain contracts check coverage window + rules + evidence hash; if valid, they trigger capped payouts in stablecoins.

Everything is driven by objective events and reproducible data—no discretionary “claims handling”.

---

## 6. Business Model & Unit Economics

**Revenue streams**

- Retail premiums: users pay for 8h / 24h / monthly coverage.  
- Revenue sharing: Binance / BNB perp partners / communities receive a share of premiums.  
- (Optional) Trading fee rebates: additional upside if integrated with volume programs.

**Risk & margins (targets)**

- Target loss ratio: ~60–70% (payouts / premiums).  
- Target gross margin: ~20–30% after risk load, ops, and partner share.  
- Risk controls: per-user and per-day caps, max payout as % of notional, waiting periods, blacklists.

---

## 7. Roadmap for Binance / BNB Chain

**Phase 1 – BNB Chain integration (0–3 months)**

- Port core liquidation-insurance logic to BNB Smart Chain.  
- Deploy initial contracts for policy storage, events, and payouts.  
- Connect to Binance read-only APIs in a BNB-focused verifier service.

**Phase 2 – Public beta (3–6 months)**

- Launch a public beta dApp on BNB Chain for retail users.  
- Offer a small set of focused products (e.g. BTCUSDT 20x / 50x 24h coverage).  
- Start with conservative limits and transparent reporting.

**Phase 3 – Deep integration & scaling (6–12 months)**

- Integrate LiqPass as an embedded module with selected Binance products / partners.  
- Expand coverage to more markets and user segments.  
- Optimize pricing and risk parameters using real-world data.

---

## 8. Why Me (Founder)

- Solo founder with full-stack ownership: frontend, backend, smart contracts, and infra.  
- Prior attempts taught me to focus on **small, verifiable loops** instead of grand, vague roadmaps.  
- I already shipped a working MVP on another chain with minimal resources.  
- Committed to turning risk management into open, auditable infrastructure—not a black box.

Handles & links:

- GitHub: https://github.com/wjz5788/LiqPass  
- Demo: https://wjz5788.com/  
- X (Twitter): @CryptoNaxin  
- Telegram: https://t.me/mosheng1u  

---

## 9. What We’re Asking From Binance

- Strategic investment (equity or token-based) to scale LiqPass as a dedicated protection layer.  
- Product and technical collaboration to embed LiqPass-style protection into Binance / BNB experiences.  
- Support on risk, data, and go-to-market so we can bring more users, more stablecoins, and more sustainable volume into the BNB ecosystem.

> “Give retail a survivable way to trade leverage; give Binance and BNB Chain a new, verifiable risk primitive.”

---

## 10. Claims & Treasury Safety (24h Settlement)

- LiqPass uses a 24h settlement window for claims instead of unconstrained instant payouts.  
- Same-day accounting, next-day settlement: approved claims are recorded immediately, paid in a 24h batch.  
- Smart contracts focus on recording, limits, and emitting events; they do **not** allow unlimited withdrawals.  
- Per-day / per-user / per-policy caps plus circuit breakers bound worst-case damage to a single settlement cycle.  
- A transparency dashboard shows 7D policies sold, loss ratio, treasury balance, daily premium vs. paid, and full event logs.  
- Even if there is a contract or logic bug, the treasury cannot be drained in one shot; the system can be paused and fixed.

*(Screenshots: Product Center → Orders → Claims → Transparency dashboard → on-chain explorer tx for a payout or premium payment.)*
```

## 二、中文对应版本（可以单独用）

```markdown
# LiqPass – 面向杠杆合约散户的爆仓保险（BNB Chain 版本）

## 1. 问题：散户做杠杆，就是“一次爆仓游戏”

- CEX 上的小资金散户普遍害怕开高倍杠杆。  
- 一次强平就可能把整个账户清空，没有一个简单、可预先预算的亏损上限。  
- 社区、KOL、量化团队想把用户带入永续合约，但缺少一个可复用、可验证的“保护模块”。  
- 对于交易所和公链来说，这意味着：成交量损失、稳定币 TVL 流失、用户生命周期很短。

---

## 2. 解决方案：LiqPass（爆仓保）

**一个面向杠杆交易者的“参数化爆仓保险”层，设计为运行在 Binance 与 BNB Chain 之间。**

- 用户在 BNB Chain 上用稳定币支付一笔小保费，购买短期保障（8 小时 / 24 小时 / 按月）。  
- 如果在保障期间内发生强平，用户提交对应的 Binance 订单号。  
- LiqPass 通过 Binance 只读 API 和线下证据流水，**被动验证**是否发生强平事件。  
- 证据包（标准化订单数据 + 哈希）会被锚定到链上，满足条件的事件会触发**规则化、有上限的赔付**。  
- 全流程基于事件和数据，可审计、可复现——不靠“截图仲裁”，也不靠人工拍脑袋。

---

## 3. 为什么对 Binance & BNB Chain 有价值

**对散户用户**

- 给小资金交易者一个清晰、可预算的亏损上限，而不是“无限下行”。  
- 把“爆仓恐惧”变成一笔他们可以承受的、事先支付的保费。  
- 让高杠杆策略变成“可存活”的玩法，而不是单局赌博。

**对 Binance & BNB Chain**

- 吸引并留住原本不敢参与杠杆的“小资金用户”。  
- 用户敢下单、敢持仓，永续合约成交量和手续费随之提升。  
- 让 USDT / USDC 以及 BNB 生态稳定币持续沉淀在 BNB Chain 上。  
- 打开一条新的分润赛道：保险保费可以在 Binance、LiqPass 和生态伙伴之间分享。

---

## 4. 产品现状快照（Today）

- **已有 MVP 在其他公链上运行**
  - 支付 / Checkout 合约已部署主网，负责接收保费并发出事件。  
  - 后端 + 验证服务已接好 CEX 只读 API。  
  - Web 端流程完整：购买保障 → 查看在保保单 → 提交理赔。

- **证据优先的设计**

  - 每一笔理赔都有标准化订单数据与衍生指标作为底层证据。  
  - 证据 → Merkle / 根哈希 → 上链锚定，方便事后审计和复算。

- 本次资助 / 合作将用于：**将 LiqPass 迁移并专项适配到 Binance & BNB Chain 生态。**

*（此处可以插入 2–3 张当前 LiqPass 的 UI 截图：产品页、订单页、理赔页。）*

---

## 5. 整体流程（高层视角）

1. **在 BNB Chain 上购买保障**  
   用户连接钱包，选择产品（例如：24h BTCUSDT 50x 保障），用稳定币支付保费。

2. **正常在 Binance 上交易**  
   用户在 Binance 上按自己的节奏做杠杆交易；LiqPass 只通过只读 API 监听，不托管资金。

3. **如果发生爆仓 / 强平**  
   用户把 Binance 的订单号提交给 LiqPass。

4. **验证与证据生成**  
   线下服务通过 Binance API 拉取订单 / 成交历史，标准化处理，生成证据包，并对结果进行签名。

5. **链上判定与赔付**  
   BNB Chain 上的合约根据保障时间窗口 + 规则 + 证据哈希进行校验，满足条件则触发有限额的稳定币赔付。

整个流程由**客观事件 + 可复现数据**驱动，而不是“人工判案”。

---

## 6. 商业模式与单位经济

**收入来源**

- 散户保费：用户为 8h / 24h / 月度保障付费。  
- 分润机制：Binance / BNB 合约生态方 / 社区伙伴从保费中获得分成。  
- （可选）交易手续费返利：与交易量计划联动时的额外收益空间。

**风险与利润（目标）**

- 目标赔付率：约 60–70%（赔付 / 保费）。  
- 目标毛利率：约 20–30%（包含风险附加、运营成本与合作方分成之后）。  
- 风控手段：单人 / 单日限额、赔付金额占名义本金的上限、等待期、黑名单等。

---

## 7. 面向 Binance / BNB Chain 的路线图

**Phase 1 – BNB Chain 集成（0–3 个月）**

- 将核心爆仓保险逻辑迁移到 BNB Smart Chain。  
- 部署首批合约：保单存储、事件记录、赔付逻辑等。  
- 搭建专门面向 Binance 的验证服务，接入 Binance 只读 API。

**Phase 2 – 公测阶段（3–6 个月）**

- 在 BNB Chain 上发布面向散户的公测 dApp。  
- 先从少量、聚焦的产品组合开始（例如：BTCUSDT 20x / 50x 24h 保障）。  
- 以保守限额和透明报表起步，逐步扩容。

**Phase 3 – 深度集成与扩展（6–12 个月）**

- 将 LiqPass 作为一个嵌入式模块，集成到部分 Binance 产品 / 合作方中。  
- 扩展支持更多交易对和用户细分人群。  
- 基于真实数据持续优化定价与风险参数。

---

## 8. 为什么是我（创始人）

- 单人负责前端、后端、智能合约与基础设施的完整链路。  
- 多次尝试之后，我更关注**“小而可验证的闭环”**，而不是空泛的大蓝图。  
- 已经在另一条公链上用极少的资源跑通了一个可用 MVP。  
- 目标是把风险管理做成**开放、可审计的基础设施**，而不是黑箱。

相关链接：

- GitHub：https://github.com/wjz5788/LiqPass  
- Demo： https://wjz5788.com/  
- X（Twitter）：@CryptoNaxin  
- Telegram： https://t.me/mosheng1u  

---

## 9. 希望 Binance 提供的支持

- 战略投资（股权或 Token 形式），用于把 LiqPass 打造成专用的“风险保护层”。  
- 产品与技术合作，将 LiqPass 式的保障能力嵌入 Binance / BNB 的使用场景中。  
- 在风险、数据、市场推广上的支持，共同把更多用户、更多稳定币和更可持续的交易量带入 BNB 生态。

> “给散户一个可以‘活下去’的杠杆交易方式；给 Binance 与 BNB Chain 一个全新的、可验证的风险原语。”

---

## 10. 理赔与资金安全（24 小时结算窗口）

- LiqPass 采用**24 小时结算窗口**，而不是完全不受约束的“实时无限额赔付”。  
- 当日记账、次日结算：通过审核的理赔会立即入账，实际转账在 24 小时批量执行。  
- 智能合约的职责集中在：记录、限额、发出事件，不提供任意金额的直接提取通道。  
- 通过单日 / 单用户 / 单保单限额以及“熔断开关”，把最坏情况控制在一个结算周期之内。  
- 公共看板展示：最近 7 天保单数、赔付率、资金池余额、每日保费 vs 赔付、完整事件日志。  
- 即便出现合约或逻辑漏洞，资金池也不可能在一次交易中被“清空”，系统可以在一个周期内暂停并修复。

```

```markdown
LiqPass turns high-leverage trading from a one-strike game into a survivable experience, using Base as the verifiable settlement layer. With a small premium, retail traders can cap their downside while LiqPass anchors liquidation evidence and payouts on Base, creating onchain volume, USDC flows, and a new “risk primitive” other Base dapps can compose with.

An MVP already exists on another chain (contracts, backend verifier, and working UI). A Base grant would directly fund: (1) porting the core contracts and evidence pipeline to Base, (2) launching a public beta for BTC/USDC leverage users, and (3) building transparent dashboards for policies, loss ratios, and treasury safety.

This is a lean, shipping-first solo founder; a relatively small grant can convert into a live, composable insurance module that improves user safety and retention across the Base ecosystem.

### 关于 “Please link a 1 minute demo of the project”

严格来说，这个字段期望的是：**1 分钟左右的视频链接**，而不是 PDF。

建议做法：

- 录一段 1 分钟屏幕录制（演示：连接钱包 → 购买保单 → 查看订单 / 理赔入口）；  
- 上传到 Loom / YouTube / Google Drive（设为 Anyone with the link 可看）；  
- 在表单的 “1 minute demo” 那一栏填这个视频链接；  
- 你的官网 https://wjz5788.com/ 和 GitHub 放在其它 “website / repo / additional links” 字段里；  
- PDF（如 pitch、路线图）可以作为**附加材料**，但不要替代这个 1 分钟 demo。  

这样既满足要求，又把你已经有的 UI 优势展示出来。

验证服务（Python + FastAPI）：已完成与 OKX / 币安的只读接口与 HMAC 校验逻辑，能生成标准化的 evidence_bundle 和 merkleRoot；演示环境暂以测试模式运行，真实日更上链锚定将在拿到稳定密钥后开启。
Verification service (Python + FastAPI): The logic for OKX/Binance read-only APIs and HMAC verification is implemented and can produce a normalized evidence_bundle and merkleRoot. The demo uses a test mode; daily on-chain anchoring with real keys will be enabled once stable key management is in place.

下一里程碑（近期）：在云服务器上跑通“小规模内测闭环”——从「云端演示版买保 → Base 上 PremiumPaid → 订单回填 → 证据归档/上链 → 透明页展示 → 手动/半自动触发赔付」，并补完对 B 端的简易分成仪表盘（GMV / 赔付率 / 分成金额）。
Next milestone (near term): Run a small internal loop fully on the cloud demo—“buy policy on the demo → PremiumPaid on Base → order backfill → evidence archiving/on-chain anchor → transparency page → manual/semi-automated payout trigger”—and ship a simple B-side rev-share dashboard (GMV, loss ratio, and revenue share).
```

这版把“现在就是云服务器演示环境、部分逻辑是 test / 人工”的事实说清楚了，又不显得寒酸，适合给 Base/投资人看：**已经能跑，下一步是把闭环做实与自动化**。

### A. 给投资人 / 基金方的 DM

**英文版：**

> Hi [Name],  
>   
> I’m building **LiqPass** – a liquidation insurance layer for high-leverage perp traders. Retail users pay a small premium, and if they get liquidated, we verify the event via CEX read-only APIs and anchor the evidence + payout onchain.  
>   
> I already have a working demo live (front-end + contracts + verifier), focused on making leverage **survivable** instead of a one-strike game.  
>   
> If you’re exploring infra around perps, risk, or stablecoin flows, LiqPass might be an interesting primitive to back early. I’d be happy to share a 1-minute demo and a short deck.  
>   
> Best,  
> [your name] – @CryptoNaxin  
> Demo: [demo link]  

**中文对应：**

> 嗨 [Name]，  
>   
> 我在做一个叫 **LiqPass** 的项目——面向高杠杆永续合约交易者的“爆仓保险层”。散户付一笔小保费，如果被强平，我们通过交易所只读 API 做验证，把证据和赔付结果锚定到链上。  
>   
> 目前已经有可运行的演示版本（前端 + 合约 + 验证服务），目标是让杠杆交易**可存活**，而不是“一次爆仓游戏”。  
>   
> 如果你在看与永续合约、风险基础设施或稳定币流动性相关的方向，LiqPass 可能是一个值得早期布局的原语。我可以发你 1 分钟 Demo 和一份精简 Deck。  
>   
> 此致，  
> [你的名字] – @CryptoNaxin  
> 演示链接： [demo link]  

---

### B. 给开发者 / 潜在合作者的 DM

**英文版：**

> Hey [Name],  
>   
> I’m building **LiqPass**, a liquidation insurance layer for high-leverage perp traders. It ties CEX read-only APIs to onchain evidence and capped payouts, so retail traders get a budgeted loss cap instead of total wipeouts.  
>   
> The current demo has front-end, a live checkout contract, and a verifier service; next steps are tightening the claim loop and scaling products. I’m looking for builders who enjoy event-driven infra, proofs, and clean risk logic (equity / rev-share, no short-term salary).  
>   
> If this resonates with what you like to build, I can share the repo + 1-minute demo for a deeper look.  
>   
> Cheers,  
> [your name] – @CryptoNaxin  
> GitHub: https://github.com/wjz5788/LiqPass  
> Demo: [demo link]  

**中文对应：**

> 嘿 [Name]，  
>   
> 我在做 **LiqPass**，一个给高杠杆永续合约交易者用的“爆仓保险层”。它把交易所只读 API、链上证据和有上限的赔付串在一起，让散户有一个可预算的亏损上限，而不是被一次强平直接清空。  
>   
> 现在的演示版本已经有前端、上线的支付合约和验证服务，下一步是把理赔闭环做扎实、再扩更多产品。我希望找一些喜欢事件驱动基础设施、证明 / Merkle 结构、以及干净风控逻辑的开发者，一起做（股权 / 分成模式，没有短期工资）。  
>   
> 如果这个方向对你有吸引力，我可以把仓库和 1 分钟 Demo 发给你，方便深入看看。  
>   
> 致敬，  
> [你的名字] – @CryptoNaxin  
> GitHub： https://github.com/wjz5788/LiqPass  
> 演示链接： [demo link]  

---

这几段都刻意控制在 DM 友好的长度：**开门见山说明你做什么 → 现在已经做到哪一步 → 你要什么 → 给出链接**。后面你可以根据对象“加一点个性化一句”，比如顺手夸一句对方之前写过的文章 / 做过的项目，把命中率再拉高一档。

<!-- mid: 6cfee016-7c37-4127-a46b-bdceeaf669eb -->
先把能直接粘进表单的英文给你，然后再配一版中文理解用。

---

### 字段一：你们在建造什么？（≤ 50 字符）

```text
LiqPass: Liquidation insurance on Arbitrum
```

---

### 字段二：Problem & Solution（英文回答）

```markdown
Problem  
High-leverage perp traders, especially small accounts, face “one-strike” liquidations: a single forced close can wipe them out. There is no simple, budgeted loss cap or verifiable protection primitive that Arbitrum-native perps, aggregators, and communities can plug into. This limits user confidence, trading volume, and stablecoin stickiness on Arbitrum.

Solution  
LiqPass is a parametric liquidation-insurance layer built to sit between CEX/Arbitrum perps and Arbitrum as the settlement layer. Users pay a small premium for short-term coverage (8h / 24h / monthly). If liquidation occurs, an off-chain verifier uses read-only APIs to confirm the event, produces a normalized evidence bundle + hash, and Arbitrum contracts enforce rule-based, capped payouts. Everything is event-driven and auditable, so Arbitrum apps can compose LiqPass as a reusable risk primitive to protect their users and grow volume.
```

---

### 中文对应（给你自己看，不用贴表单）

- **问题**：高杠杆永续合约用户（尤其是小资金）基本是“一次爆仓游戏”，没有简单、可预算的亏损上限，也没有可复用、可验证的“保险模块”给 Arbitrum 自家 perp/聚合器/社区调用。  
- **解决方案**：LiqPass 在 CEX/Arbitrum perp 和 Arbitrum 链之间加一层“参数化爆仓保险”：用户付小保费买短期保障，爆仓后由验证服务通过只读 API 拉数据、生成证据与哈希，链上合约按规则+上限赔付，事件驱动、可审计，又方便 Arbitrum 生态里的各类 dApp 直接组合使用。

### Describe the competition and or the alternatives and why you are better *

建议答案（控制在一小段里）：

```markdown
Most existing “insurance” options for perp traders fall into three buckets: (1) protocol- or CEX-level insurance funds and ADL systems, which are opaque and don’t give a clear, per-user loss cap; (2) DeFi insurance platforms (e.g. protocol exploit cover) that don’t handle per-position liquidation events; and (3) self-hedging strategies that are too complex for small retail accounts.

LiqPass focuses narrowly on parametric liquidation cover: short-term windows, objective liquidation events, normalized evidence, and onchain, rule-based capped payouts. It is designed as a reusable module Arbitrum-native perps and aggregators can plug into, with transparent 24h settlement limits and evidence anchored onchain, instead of a black-box insurance fund.
```

> 中文要点：  
> 现有方案要么是黑箱保险基金/ADL，要么是只保“合约被黑”的通用保险，要么是复杂的自我对冲；LiqPass 专注“爆仓这一个事件”，参数化、可验证、可组合，而且赔付规则和限额清晰。

### Referral（推荐人）

中文意思：

如果这个字段是可选的，你也可以留空，但我更建议按上面那样写一句，显示你是认真填表的。

---

### Acknowledgement（确认勾选）

这两条都是**打勾的 checkbox**，没有文字输入：  

- 第一条是长篇法律话：  
  - 你知道审计是三方公司做的，不是基金会本身做；  
  - 你同意他们看你提交的信息并转给审计公司；  
  - 通过邮件联系你；  
  - 以及一堆责任豁免条款。  
  👉 **你要申请，就必须勾。**

- **Acknowledgment 2： audited code 在一段时间内只属于 Arbitrum（exclusive to Arbitrum for a set period of time）**

  这句话的实际含义大致是：  
  > 用这笔 Arbitrum 资助做安全审计的那部分代码，在约定的一段时间里，算是 “Arbitrum 优先”，你不能马上把**同一份审计结果**原样拿去其他链当成“也被审过”的卖点。

  对你来说，比较务实的做法是：  
  - 把这次审计定位为 **“Arbitrum 版本合约的审计”**；  
  - Base 那边要么继续用你现在的 MVP 版本，要么后面如果真要审计，再单独搞一轮；  
  - 即：**Arbitrum = 主力、被审计的版本**，其他链当“移植/复用逻辑”，不要写成“共享一份审计”。

你如果接受这个游戏规则（换来免费/补贴审计），就直接把两个勾全勾上，然后提交表单就行。  

接下来真正重要的，是把你这套“Arbitrum 版本合约”和当前架构整理好，等他们来问细节时，你能秒丢代码和 Demo 链接，让他们感受到：这项目是能跑的，不是 PPT。

### 2. AI App 基本信息

```text
LiqPass Risk Agent – Arbitrum Perp Protector
```

**Link to AI Agent/App \***  
这里需要你准备一个可以访问的链接（比如你自己网站上的一个页面、Notion 页面、或简单前端 Demo）。先给你模板：

```text

```

> 你可以之后在 wjz5788.com 上挂一个简单“Agent Demo”页，把这个链接填进去。

---

### 3. Description of What You Built/Building \*  
（简介：做了什么）

```markdown
LiqPass Risk Agent is an Arbitrum-focused DeFi agent that helps high-leverage perp traders manage liquidation risk. The agent monitors a user’s positions, risk tolerance, and market conditions, then suggests actions such as adjusting leverage, reducing exposure, or purchasing parametric liquidation cover via LiqPass. It is built on top of Vibekit as an Arbitrum-native MCP agent, so it can call on-chain data, simulate outcomes, and return human-readable, step-by-step recommendations rather than opaque “black box” trades.
```

---

### 4. AI 架构 & 决策逻辑 \*  
（technical + decision-making architecture）

```markdown
Technically, the agent is built with Vibekit as the MCP layer, a TypeScript backend, and an LLM core. Vibekit tools expose Arbitrum RPC calls, LiqPass smart contracts, and basic analytics (e.g., current leverage, distance to liquidation, PnL). 

Decision-wise, the agent follows a fixed loop:
(1) read user context and wallet/position data;
(2) compute risk metrics (e.g., max drawdown, liquidation price buffer);
(3) check LiqPass product availability and limits;
(4) generate 2–3 candidate actions with pros/cons;
(5) present an explanation and only execute on-chain if the user confirms.  

The process is reproducible because each step is logged (inputs, tool calls, outputs), and the reasoning is constrained by a simple decision tree (risk bands → recommended actions).
```

---

### 5. Off-chain / On-chain 交互 \*  

```markdown
Off-chain, the agent runs the LLM + Vibekit stack, pulling data from Arbitrum RPC, price feeds, and LiqPass APIs. It normalizes positions, calculates liquidation risk, and decides whether to suggest hedging or purchasing cover. 

On-chain, the agent only interacts via clearly scoped calls: reading Arbitrum state (positions, balances), and—if the user approves—calling LiqPass contracts to buy coverage or read coverage status. All state-changing transactions are prepared off-chain with full explanations and then signed by the user in their own wallet, so the agent never has custody of funds.
```

---

### 6. 隐私 / 信息泄露防护 \*  

```markdown
User input and conversation logs are treated as sensitive. We avoid storing raw prompts with identifiable data; instead, we store minimized summaries and anonymized metrics (e.g., “wallet A, risk band high”) for debugging. Any stored data is kept server-side with access control, not shared with third parties. 

The agent never echoes secrets (API keys, seed phrases) and actively warns users not to paste them. For state-changing actions, the only thing leaving the private context is a prepared transaction payload that the user signs locally in their wallet, so their private information and keys never touch the agent infrastructure.
```

---

### 7. 第三方 Agent 平台 \*

## 1️⃣ Arbitrum 审计计划（Audit Program）

**是什么：**  
12 个月内拿出 10M+ ARB，补贴早期项目做第三方智能合约审计，是目前行业里最大规模的审计补贴之一。citeturn2search0turn2search12turn2search17  

**你符不符合：**

- 方向：你是 **DeFi / 风险基础设施 / 智能合约**，方向✅  
- 阶段：他们偏好**已经有一定产品形态和增长潜力**的早期项目，而不是纯 PPT。你有 Base MVP + 正在做 Arbitrum 版本，这一条是能讲得通的。  
- 前提：得有 **部署在 Arbitrum 上的核心合约**，以及一个大致稳定的 v1 范围（审哪个合约、哪部分逻辑）。

👉 结论：**这个是你的核心目标之一，可以申请，而且很适配。**  
顺序上：等你把“Arbitrum 版 LiqPass 合约”敲定一版，再正式提交（我们之前已经帮你填了一套表单答案，可以继续用）。

---

## 2️⃣ ArbiFuel（Gas 赞助计划）

**是什么：**  
给早期 Arbitrum 项目提供 Gas 赞助，让你在上线初期可以多测试、多引导用户，而不用太心疼 gas。项目会为每个团队提供一定上限的 gas 额度。citeturn2search1turn2search18turn2search23turn2search28  

**你符不符合：**

- 条件：只要你是**在 Arbitrum 上部署合约、要让真实用户来交互**的早期项目，基本就符合申请逻辑。  
- 用法：非常适合你在 Arbitrum 上做「小规模内测 / 邀请用户试用 LiqPass」阶段，帮用户垫一部分 gas（比如买保单、发起理赔、调用透明页面等）。

👉 结论：**适合你在 Arbitrum 有初步前端 + 合约之后申请**，用来撑起“第一批真实用户”的交互成本。

---

## 3️⃣ Trailblazer 2.0（Vibekit DeFi Agent 资助）

**是什么：**  
1M 美金专门给：用 **Vibekit（Ember 做的 Arbitrum 原生 MCP agent 框架）** 搭建的 DeFi Agent、Agent 模板、工具。每个项目大概上限 ~1 万美金左右，重点看“真实实用、有价值的 agent”。citeturn2search2turn2search3turn2search29turn2search34  

**你符不符合：**

- 从方向看：✅  
  - 你做的是“杠杆爆仓风险 + 保险”，很适合做一个 **LiqPass Risk Agent**：看仓位、算风险、建议减仓/买保险，是典型 DeFi agent 场景。  
- 从现实进度看：  
  - 你**还没真正用 Vibekit 把 agent 跑起来**，现在只有架构设想和我们写好的表单草稿。

👉 结论：

- **现在立刻申请 → 说不太过去；** 他们会问：GitHub？Demo？Agent 架构？  
- **只要你花一段时间，做出最小可用的 Vibekit Agent**（能调用 Arbitrum RPC / 合约、计算风险、给出建议），你就很适合冲这项，而且叙事很好讲：  
  > “LiqPass 是合约层的风险保护，LiqPass Agent 是用户层/策略层的智能助手。”

---

## 4️⃣ Arbitrum DAO 资助计划（DAO Grants）

**是什么：**  
Arbitrum DAO 这边的长期资助：面向在 Arbitrum One / Nova / Orbit 上构建、且有清晰里程碑的项目，按 milestone 给钱，重视 adoption、infra、社区等。citeturn2search3turn2search6turn2search10turn2search25  

**你符不符合：**

- 方向：你是 DeFi 协议 + 风险基础设施，符合“新协议 / 基础设施”类型。  
- 阶段：他们更希望你有：
  - 已经在 Arbitrum 上跑起来的东西  
  - 一些数据：用户数、交易量、保费与赔付、合作方  
  - 一套清楚的“用这笔钱做哪些里程碑”的 roadmap  

👉 结论：**中期目标**。  
先搞定：Arbitrum 部署 + 小规模用户 + 合约审计 / 安全故事，再来写 DAO Grants，会比现在直接冲靠谱很多。

---

## 5️⃣ Arbitrum Gaming Ventures（AGV）

**是什么：**  
专门投 **游戏 / 娱乐 / 互动内容** 的 200M ARB 战略计划，给做游戏、游戏基础设施、娱乐内容的团队投钱或给 grant。citeturn2search4turn2search16turn2search26turn2search31turn2search8turn2search9  

**你符不符合：**

- 你现在是：**杠杆爆仓保险，偏 DeFi / Infra，不是游戏。**  

👉 结论：**目前基本无关，直接忽略。**  
只有你以后真想玩「游戏化风控 / 模拟交易游戏 / 教育向交互游戏」这种重度 pivot，才有可能考虑。

---

## 6️⃣ Alchemy–Arbitrum Orbit Grant（炼金术 Orbit 资助）

**是什么：**  
Alchemy + Arbitrum 联合的 10M 美金 Orbit 链资助：  
给那些要 **基于 Arbitrum Orbit 搭自己的 rollup / L3 链** 的团队，每个团队最多 50 万美金等值的基础设施 credits。citeturn3search0turn3search1turn3search2turn3search3turn3search7  

**你符不符合：**

- 你现在的设计：  
  - 是一个部署在 L2（Base / 将来的 Arbitrum One）上的 DeFi 协议 + off-chain verifier。  
  - **还没有要自己开一条 Orbit 链（专门的 appchain）的计划**。  

👉 结论：  
- **短期内没必要去碰。**  
- 以后如果你觉得：“LiqPass 要做成一个高吞吐的风险结算专用链（比如给多家 CEX / 协议统一清算证据和赔付）”，那才是 Orbit + Alchemy 这类 grant 的舞台。

---

## 帮你排个优先级（针对 LiqPass 现状）

按你现在这个阶段，我建议这样排：

1. **Arbitrum 审计计划（Audit Program）**  
   - ✅ 方向完全匹配  
   - 等 Arbitrum 合约 v1 定下来，就可以严肃申请（我们已经起草过一版回答）。

2. **ArbiFuel（Gas 赞助）**  
   - 在你把 LiqPass 部署到 Arbitrum、准备让真实用户来试的时候申请，帮你解决“首批用户的 gas 成本”。

3. **Trailblazer 2.0（Vibekit / DeFi Agent）**  
   - 先把 **最小版 LiqPass Risk Agent** 做出来（用 Vibekit），再申请，这一项对你很加分。

4. **Arbitrum DAO Grants**  
   - 等你在 Arbitrum 有：  
     - 真用户  
     - 一些数据  
     - 初步安全故事（审计 / review）  
   再用“扩展产品线 + 生态集成”角度去要一笔更大的里程碑式资助。

5. **Gaming Ventures / Orbit Grant**  
   - 目前直接忽略，当作**远期选项**：只有你要做游戏或专用链时再看。

---

一句话收尾：  
**LiqPass + Arbitrum = 风险基础设施**，你现在要做的是：  
把“产品能跑” + “安全有保障” + “用户敢用”三件事和这几个资金池一一对上，  
优先拿下 Audit + ArbiFuel，等 agent 出来再去敲 Trailblazer 的门。

## 3. 你可以怎么包装 LiqPass 给 “New Protocols 3.0” 看？

给你一个简化版叙事骨架（之后可以填进他们的 Questbook 表单）：

**What is the project?**

> LiqPass is a parametric liquidation insurance protocol for high-leverage perp traders on Arbitrum. Users pay a small premium for short-term coverage (8h / 24h / monthly). If liquidation occurs, an off-chain verifier confirms the event via read-only CEX / perp data and Arbitrum contracts enforce rule-based, capped payouts. It becomes a reusable “risk primitive” that Arbitrum-native perps and aggregators can plug into.

**Why does it matter for Arbitrum?**

> It turns high-leverage trading from a one-strike game into a survivable experience, especially for small accounts. That increases perp volume, improves stablecoin stickiness, and gives other Arbitrum protocols an opt-in protection layer they can offer to their users.

**What will you do with the grant?**

例子（4–6 个月里程碑）：

1. 在 **Arbitrum One 部署 LiqPass v1 合约**（保单登记 + PremiumPaid + 赔付限额 + Merkle 证据锚定）  
2. 做一个面向 Arbitrum 的前端 Demo（连接钱包 → 购买 24h 爆仓保 → 查看透明页面）

5. 准备后续审计（可以配合 Arbitrum Audit Program 一起讲）

---

不搞虚的，给你一条可执行路线：

4. 然后你再看：  
   - 审计 Program（安全）  
   - ArbiFuel（Gas）  
   - Trailblazer 2.0（Agent）

---

最后总结一句：  
你贴的这些数据，不是吓人的，是在告诉你——**Arbitrum DAO 目前钱还挺多，尤其在「New Protocols and Ideas」这一栏**。  
LiqPass 这种专门做“爆仓保险 / 风险基础设施”的项目，在他们眼里是「新类型协议」，比大部分 Meme 借贷 clone 更像他们想扶持的东西。你现在要做的，是用一份干净的 Proposal 把这点讲清楚。

## 二、Grant Information（项目信息）

### Title – Name of your Project (0/80)

```text
LiqPass – Parametric Liquidation Insurance on Arbitrum
```

---

### Project Details  
> Describe your project/idea, brief and concise

```markdown
LiqPass is a parametric liquidation insurance protocol for high-leverage perp traders on Arbitrum. Users pay a small premium for short-term coverage (8h / 24h / monthly). If a forced liquidation occurs during the coverage window, an off-chain verifier confirms the event via read-only data and Arbitrum contracts enforce rule-based, capped payouts.

The goal is to turn leverage from a “one-strike liquidation game” into a survivable experience, especially for small accounts, and to give Arbitrum-native perps and aggregators a reusable risk primitive they can plug into.
```

---

### What innovation or value will your project bring to Arbitrum?  

```markdown
Most current “insurance” in perps is opaque: protocol- or CEX-level insurance funds, ADL systems, or generalized DeFi cover that doesn’t target per-position liquidations. Retail users still lack a simple, budgeted loss cap, and Arbitrum protocols lack a composable liquidation-protection module.

LiqPass focuses narrowly on parametric liquidation cover: short windows, objective liquidation events, normalized evidence, and onchain, rule-based capped payouts. Evidence bundles (e.g., Merkle roots) are anchored on Arbitrum, and policies/payouts are fully transparent.

For Arbitrum, this provides:
- A new DeFi primitive for perps, options, and structured products;
- Stronger user safety and confidence for high-leverage trading;
- Improved perp volume and stablecoin stickiness, as users can survive more than one bad trade.
```

---

### Do you have a target audience? If so, which one?  

```markdown
Yes.

Primary: DeFi users on Arbitrum who trade perp futures or high-leverage products (especially small-account retail users and power users who manage multiple accounts).

Secondary: Protocols and integrators—Arbitrum-native perp/derivatives protocols, DEX aggregators, copy trading platforms, and wallets that want to offer an opt-in liquidation protection layer to their users.

Segment labels: DeFi users, perp traders, protocol integrators.
```

---

### Team experience and completeness  

```markdown
LiqPass is currently built by a solo founder (zms), acting as Founder & Technical Lead.

Roles and responsibilities:
- Smart contracts: design and implementation of liquidation insurance contracts (policy registry, payout logic, evidence anchoring).
- Backend: TypeScript/Express + SQLite event-driven backend, syncing onchain events with off-chain records.
- Verification service: Python/FastAPI service to normalize liquidation evidence and produce Merkle roots / signed results.
- Front-end: React/TypeScript web app for buying coverage, viewing policies, and claims UI.
- Infra: cloud deployment, monitoring, and CI.

Relevant experience:
- Shipped a working MVP on another L2 (Base): checkout contract live, backend + verifier integrated, and a demo web app.
- Strong focus on evidence-first design (normalized data + Merkle roots) and event-driven accounting.

References:
- GitHub codebase: https://github.com/wjz5788/LiqPass  
- Live demo: https://wjz5788.com/
```

---

### Do you know about any comparable protocol…?  

```markdown
Comparable/adjacent projects include:

- Protocol insurance funds and ADL mechanisms on perp DEXs (e.g., internal insurance funds on major perp platforms), which are opaque and not user-selectable.
- General DeFi cover protocols (e.g., Nexus Mutual, InsurAce, Risk Harbor) that focus on protocol exploits or depegs rather than per-position liquidation events.
- Self-hedging and structured products that require active management and are often too complex for small accounts.

To our knowledge, there is no Arbitrum-native, parametric liquidation insurance primitive that:
(1) targets per-position liquidation,
(2) is composable by multiple protocols, and
(3) anchors evidence and payouts transparently on Arbitrum.
```

---

### What is the current stage of your project?  

```markdown
MVP live on another L2 (Base), pre-launch on Arbitrum.

- Base: checkout contract deployed, backend + verifier integrated, and a demo front-end running on a cloud server.
- Arbitrum: contract design adapted, with this grant aimed at implementing, deploying, and testing the Arbitrum-first version of LiqPass.
```

---

```

```

---

## 三、Grant request details（资助细节）

### What is the idea/project for which you are applying for a grant  

```markdown
We are applying to build and launch **LiqPass v1 on Arbitrum One**: a parametric liquidation insurance protocol natively deployed on Arbitrum.

Implementation plan:
1. Implement and deploy core LiqPass contracts on Arbitrum:
   - Policy registry and coverage windows,
   - Premium checkout and PremiumPaid events,
   - Payout logic with per-user/per-day caps,
   - Evidence hash anchoring (Merkle root + attestor signatures).
2. Build and deploy an Arbitrum-focused backend and verifier integration:
   - Event-driven backend for syncing policies and payouts,
   - Evidence pipeline for per-position liquidation checks.
3. Ship a public alpha dApp on Arbitrum:
   - Simple UI to buy short-term cover, view active policies, and see a transparency dashboard.
4. Run a small, controlled pilot with early users and at least one Arbitrum perp/aggregator integration, and publish a public report on usage, loss ratios, and lessons learned.
```

---

### Outline the major deliverables you will obtain with this grant  

```markdown
1. **LiqPass v1 contracts on Arbitrum One**
   - Policy registry, PremiumPaid events, capped payout logic, and evidence anchoring.
   - Publicly verified on Arbiscan.

2. **Backend + verifier integration for Arbitrum**
   - Event-driven backend that records policies and payouts from Arbitrum.
   - Evidence pipeline wired to handle liquidation checks for pilot integrations.

3. **Public alpha dApp**
   - Deployed front-end for buying cover, viewing policies, and accessing a transparency dashboard (policies sold, loss ratio, treasury balance).

4. **Pilot integration and report**
   - Limited rollout to early users / partner protocol(s).
   - Public report summarizing metrics, risk performance, and next steps.
```

---

### Alignment with Arbitrum and growth impact  

```markdown
LiqPass directly supports Arbitrum’s DeFi and ecosystem expansion goals by:

- **DeFi dominance**: It gives perp protocols, structured products, and aggregators an optional liquidation protection layer, helping onboard users who are currently afraid of high leverage because of “one-strike” liquidations.
- **Stablecoin stickiness**: Stablecoins used to pay premiums and receive payouts stay within Arbitrum, reinforcing onchain liquidity and capital efficiency.
- **Composability**: LiqPass is designed as a primitive—protocols can integrate it at the UI or smart contract level, offering users opt-in protection and sharing premium revenue.
- **Risk transparency**: The transparency dashboard and evidence anchoring make risk more auditable, which is important for institutions and sophisticated DeFi users.

By making high-leverage strategies more survivable, LiqPass can increase perp volume, user retention, and protocol differentiation on Arbitrum.
```

---

### What is your requested grant (USD)?  

我建议你要一个**适中、容易批**的数：  

```text
20,000 USD
```

---

### Budget breakdown  

```markdown
Total requested: 20,000 USD

1) Smart contract development & testing – 6,000 USD  
   - Finalizing Arbitrum-specific LiqPass v1 contracts  
   - Test suite, fuzzing, and internal reviews before external audit

2) Backend & verifier integration – 4,000 USD  
   - Arbitrum event-driven backend (policy registry, payouts)  
   - Evidence pipeline wiring for liquidation checks  

3) Front-end & transparency dashboard – 4,000 USD  
   - Arbitrum-focused dApp (policy purchase, policy list)  
   - Dashboard for policies, loss ratios, and treasury metrics  

4) Infra & operations – 3,000 USD  
   - Cloud hosting, monitoring, logging, Arbitrum RPC providers  
   - Staging and production environments

5) Pilot incentives & user support – 3,000 USD  
   - Seed treasury / test coverage budget for early users  
   - Support, documentation, and integration assistance for pilot partners
```

Deliverables:

- Migration to Arbitrum One mainnet with verified contracts on Arbiscan.
- Backend service listening to PremiumPaid events and storing policy records in a database.
```

- **Deadline:**  
 选择大概：从现在算起 2 个月内的日期（例如 2026-02-15）。

```

Ship a public alpha dApp on Arbitrum and a basic transparency dashboard.

Deliverables:

- Transparency dashboard with: number of policies sold, basic loss ratio, and treasury balance (updated at least daily).
- Documentation for users and integrators explaining how LiqPass works and how evidence/payouts are handled.
```

大约第 4 个月（例如 2026-04-15）。

7,000 USD
```

---
